class HeadlessDecisionState:
    hitler_intervention_stage = None
    hitler_intervention_target = None
    hitler_intervention_redeployments = 0
    @classmethod
    def reset_hitler_intervention(cls):
        cls.hitler_intervention_stage = None
        cls.hitler_intervention_target = None
        cls.hitler_intervention_redeployments = 0
HITLER_INTERVENTION_ACTIONS = 21
def get_total_actions():
    all_map_spaces = []
    seen_spaces = set()
    for space in get_all_map_spaces_excluding_boxes():
        if id(space) in seen_spaces:
            continue
        seen_spaces.add(id(space))
        all_map_spaces.append(space)
    german_eligible_spaces = [
        space
        for space in all_map_spaces
        if space.terrain not in [
            TerrainType.START_BOX,
            TerrainType.BEACH,
        ]
    ]
    actions = (
        7
        + len(panzer_divisions_list) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
        + len(panzer_divisions_list)
        + len(OTHER_RESERVE_UNIT_TYPES) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
        + 1  
        + 3  
        + len(german_eligible_spaces)  
        + len(MOVE_UNIT_TYPES) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
        + len(panzer_divisions_list)  
    )
    return actions, german_eligible_spaces
TOTAL_ACTIONS, german_eligible_spaces = get_total_actions()
def print_action_phase_action_mask(mask, legal_only=False):
    action_names = []
    action_names.append("PASS")
    for army in armies_list:
        action_names.append(
            f"COUNTER-ATTACK: {army.name}"
        )
    for panzer in panzer_divisions_list:
        for space in german_eligible_spaces:
            action_names.append(
                f"FROM STRATEGIC RESERVE: "
                f"{panzer.name} -> {space.name}"
            )
    for panzer in panzer_divisions_list:
        action_names.append(
            f"TO STRATEGIC RESERVE: {panzer.name}"
        )
    for unit_name, _, _ in OTHER_RESERVE_UNIT_TYPES:
        for space in german_eligible_spaces:
            action_names.append(
                f"FROM STRATEGIC RESERVE: "
                f"{unit_name} -> {space.name}"
            )
    for unit_name, _, _ in MOVE_UNIT_TYPES:
        for space in german_eligible_spaces:
            action_names.append(
                f"MOVE UNIT ONE SPACE: "
                f"{unit_name} -> {space.name}"
            )
    for panzer in panzer_divisions_list:
        action_names.append(
            f"REFIT PANZER DIVISION: {panzer.name}"
        )
    action_names.append(
        "MOVE ACTION POINT TO STRATEGIC RESERVE"
    )
    action_names.append("AUGMENTATION ROLL: TRANSPORT")
    action_names.append("AUGMENTATION ROLL: SUPPLY")
    action_names.append("AUGMENTATION ROLL: HITLER APPROVAL")
    for space in german_eligible_spaces:
        action_names.append(
            f"BUILD / UPGRADE FORTIFIED VILLAGES: {space.name}"
        )
    assert len(action_names) == len(mask), (
        f"Action-name count ({len(action_names)}) "
        f"does not match mask size ({len(mask)})"
    )
    print()
    print("AI ACTION PHASE MASK")
    print("--------------------")
    for action_id, is_legal in enumerate(mask):
        if legal_only and is_legal != 1.0:
            continue
        print(
            f"{action_id}: "
            f"{action_names[action_id]} "
            f"[{is_legal:.1f}]"
        )
    print()
def get_pass_action_mask(is_legal):
    mask = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float32)
    if is_legal:
        mask[0] = 1.0
    return mask
def get_counter_attack_mask():
    mask = np.zeros(len(armies_list), dtype=np.float32)
    legal_options = get_counter_attack_options()
    options_by_army_name = {
        option["army"].name: option
        for option in legal_options
    }
    for army_index, army in enumerate(armies_list):
        if army.name in options_by_army_name:
            mask[army_index] = 1.0
    return mask
def get_move_panzer_from_strategic_reserve_mask():
    mask_size = (
        len(panzer_divisions_list)
        * len(german_eligible_spaces)
    )
    mask = np.zeros(mask_size, dtype=np.float32)
    german_controlled_spaces = get_german_controlled_spaces()
    panzer_divisions_in_reserve = (
        get_panzer_divisions_in_strategic_reserve()
    )
    for panzer_index, panzer in enumerate(
        panzer_divisions_list
    ):
        if panzer not in panzer_divisions_in_reserve:
            continue
        for space_index, space in enumerate(
            german_eligible_spaces
        ):
            if space not in german_controlled_spaces:
                continue
            local_action_id = (
                panzer_index
                * len(german_eligible_spaces)
                + space_index
            )
            mask[local_action_id] = 1.0
    return mask
def get_move_panzer_to_strategic_reserve_mask():
    mask = np.zeros(
        len(panzer_divisions_list),
        dtype=np.float32
    )
    panzer_divisions_on_map = [
        panzer
        for _, panzer in get_panzer_divisions_on_map()
    ]
    for panzer_index, panzer in enumerate(
        panzer_divisions_list
    ):
        if panzer in panzer_divisions_on_map:
            mask[panzer_index] = 1.0
    return mask
def get_move_other_unit_from_strategic_reserve_mask():
    mask_size = (
        len(OTHER_RESERVE_UNIT_TYPES)
        * len(german_eligible_spaces)
    )
    mask = np.zeros(mask_size, dtype=np.float32)
    german_controlled_spaces = get_german_controlled_spaces()
    other_units_in_reserve = (
        get_other_units_in_strategic_reserve()
    )
    for unit_type_index, (_, unit_type, named_unit) in enumerate(
        OTHER_RESERVE_UNIT_TYPES
    ):
        if named_unit is not None:
            available_unit = next(
                (
                    unit
                    for unit in other_units_in_reserve
                    if unit is named_unit
                ),
                None
            )
        else:
            available_unit = next(
                (
                    unit
                    for unit in other_units_in_reserve
                    if unit.type == unit_type
                ),
                None
            )
        if available_unit is None:
            continue
        for space_index, space in enumerate(
            german_eligible_spaces
        ):
            if space not in german_controlled_spaces:
                continue
            if not can_add_unit_to_space(
                space,
                available_unit
            ):
                continue
            local_action_id = (
                unit_type_index
                * len(german_eligible_spaces)
                + space_index
            )
            mask[local_action_id] = 1.0
    return mask
def get_move_action_point_to_strategic_reserve_mask():
    mask = np.zeros(
        1,
        dtype=np.float32
    )
    if (
        GlobalGameState.actions_left_this_turn >= 1
        and GlobalGameState.reserve_actions < 2
    ):
        mask[0] = 1.0
    return mask
def get_fortified_villages_mask():
    mask = np.zeros(len(german_eligible_spaces), dtype=np.float32)
    valid_spaces = get_fortified_village_options()
    for space_index, space in enumerate(german_eligible_spaces):
        if space in valid_spaces:
            mask[space_index] = 1.0
    return mask
def get_refit_panzer_division_mask():
    mask = np.zeros(
        len(panzer_divisions_list),
        dtype=np.float32
    )
    for panzer_index, panzer in enumerate(panzer_divisions_list):
        if (
            panzer in strategic_reserve_box.units
            and panzer.combat_value == 1
        ):
            mask[panzer_index] = 1.0
    return mask
def get_move_unit_one_space_mask():
    mask_size = len(MOVE_UNIT_TYPES) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
    mask = np.zeros(mask_size, dtype=np.float32)
    german_controlled_spaces = get_german_controlled_spaces()
    for unit_type_index, (_, unit_type, named_unit) in enumerate(MOVE_UNIT_TYPES):
        for current_space in german_controlled_spaces:
            if named_unit is not None:
                matching_units = [
                    unit
                    for unit in current_space.units
                    if unit is named_unit
                ]
            else:
                matching_units = [
                    unit
                    for unit in current_space.units
                    if isinstance(unit, GermanUnit)
                    and unit.type == unit_type
                ]
            if not matching_units:
                continue
            destination_spaces = get_adjacent_german_controlled_spaces(current_space)
            for space_index, destination_space in enumerate(german_eligible_spaces):
                if destination_space not in destination_spaces:
                    continue
                if not any(
                    can_add_unit_to_space(destination_space, unit)
                    for unit in matching_units
                ):
                    continue
                local_action_id = (
                    unit_type_index * len(german_eligible_spaces)
                    + space_index
                )
                mask[local_action_id] = 1.0
    return mask
def get_action_phase_action_mask():
    normal_ap = GlobalGameState.actions_left_this_turn
    reserve_ap = GlobalGameState.reserve_actions
    total_ap = normal_ap + reserve_ap
    has_normal_ap = normal_ap > 0
    has_available_ap = total_ap > 0
    pass_mask = get_pass_action_mask(is_legal=not has_normal_ap)
    if has_available_ap:
        counter_attack_mask = get_counter_attack_mask()
        panzer_from_reserve_mask = get_move_panzer_from_strategic_reserve_mask()
        panzer_to_reserve_mask = get_move_panzer_to_strategic_reserve_mask()
        move_unit_one_space_mask = get_move_unit_one_space_mask()
        refit_panzer_division_mask = get_refit_panzer_division_mask()
        if np.any(refit_panzer_division_mask):
            eligible_panzers = [
                panzer.name
                for panzer_index, panzer in enumerate(panzer_divisions_list)
                if refit_panzer_division_mask[panzer_index] == 1.0
            ]
            print(
                f"REFIT AVAILABLE: {', '.join(eligible_panzers)}"
            )
        augmentation_mask = augmentation_roll_mask()
    else:
        counter_attack_mask = np.zeros(
            len(armies_list),
            dtype=np.float32
        )
        panzer_from_reserve_mask = np.zeros(
            len(panzer_divisions_list) * len(german_eligible_spaces),
            dtype=np.float32
        )
        panzer_to_reserve_mask = np.zeros(
            len(panzer_divisions_list),
            dtype=np.float32
        )
        move_unit_one_space_mask = np.zeros(
            len(MOVE_UNIT_TYPES) * len(german_eligible_spaces),
            dtype=np.float32
        )
        refit_panzer_division_mask = np.zeros(
            len(panzer_divisions_list),
            dtype=np.float32
        )
        augmentation_mask = np.zeros(
            3,
            dtype=np.float32
        )
    other_unit_from_reserve_mask = get_move_other_unit_from_strategic_reserve_mask()
    move_action_point_to_reserve_mask = get_move_action_point_to_strategic_reserve_mask()
    if total_ap >= 3:
        fortified_villages_mask = get_fortified_villages_mask()
    else:
        fortified_villages_mask = np.zeros(
            len(german_eligible_spaces),
            dtype=np.float32
        )
    mask = np.concatenate(
        (
            pass_mask,
            counter_attack_mask,
            panzer_from_reserve_mask,
            panzer_to_reserve_mask,
            other_unit_from_reserve_mask,
            move_unit_one_space_mask,
            refit_panzer_division_mask,
            move_action_point_to_reserve_mask,
            augmentation_mask,
            fortified_villages_mask,
        )
    )
    return mask
def augmentation_roll_mask():
    mask = np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)
    if transport_track.value < transport_track.maximum:
        mask[0] = 1.0
    if supply_track.value < supply_track.maximum:
        mask[1] = 1.0
    if hitler_approval_track.value < hitler_approval_track.maximum:
        mask[2] = 1.0
    return mask
def get_action_mask():
    mask = np.zeros(TOTAL_ACTIONS, dtype=np.float32)
    mask[0] = 1.0
    return mask
def execute_flat_action_ai(action_id, target_option=None):
    if action_id == 0:
        GlobalGameState.actions_left_this_turn = 0
        return True
    if 1 <= action_id <= 6:
        if target_option is None:
            selected_army = armies_list[action_id - 1]
            target_option = next(
                (
                    option
                    for option in get_counter_attack_options()
                    if option["army"] is selected_army
                ),
                None
            )
        if target_option is None:
            return False
        do_counter_attack(selected_option=target_option)
        return True
    panzer_action_start = 7
    panzer_action_end = (
        panzer_action_start
        + len(panzer_divisions_list) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
    )
    if panzer_action_start <= action_id < panzer_action_end:
        relative_id = action_id - panzer_action_start
        panzer_index = relative_id // len(german_eligible_spaces)
        space_index = relative_id % len(german_eligible_spaces)
        selected_panzer = panzer_divisions_list[panzer_index]
        selected_space = german_eligible_spaces[space_index]
        do_move_panzer_from_strategic_reserve(
            randint(1, 6),
            div_choice=selected_panzer,
            space_choice=selected_space
        )
        return True
    panzer_to_reserve_start = panzer_action_end
    panzer_to_reserve_end = (
        panzer_to_reserve_start
        + len(panzer_divisions_list)
    )
    if panzer_to_reserve_start <= action_id < panzer_to_reserve_end:
        panzer_index = action_id - panzer_to_reserve_start
        selected_panzer = panzer_divisions_list[panzer_index]
        do_move_panzer_to_strategic_reserve(
            randint(1, 6),
            div_choice=selected_panzer
        )
        return True
    other_reserve_start = panzer_to_reserve_end
    other_reserve_end = (
        other_reserve_start
        + len(OTHER_RESERVE_UNIT_TYPES) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
    )
    if other_reserve_start <= action_id < other_reserve_end:
        relative_id = action_id - other_reserve_start
        unit_type_index = relative_id // len(german_eligible_spaces)
        space_index = relative_id % len(german_eligible_spaces)
        _, unit_type, named_unit = OTHER_RESERVE_UNIT_TYPES[unit_type_index]
        selected_space = german_eligible_spaces[space_index]
        if named_unit is not None:
            selected_unit = named_unit
            if selected_unit not in strategic_reserve_box.units:
                return False
        else:
            selected_unit = next(
                (
                    unit
                    for unit in strategic_reserve_box.units
                    if isinstance(unit, GermanUnit)
                    and unit.type == unit_type
                ),
                None
            )
            if selected_unit is None:
                return False
        return do_move_other_unit_from_strategic_reserve(
            unit_choice=selected_unit,
            space_choice=selected_space,
        )
    move_unit_one_space_start = other_reserve_end
    move_unit_one_space_end = (
        move_unit_one_space_start
        + len(MOVE_UNIT_TYPES) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
    )
    if move_unit_one_space_start <= action_id < move_unit_one_space_end:
        relative_id = action_id - move_unit_one_space_start
        unit_type_index = relative_id // len(german_eligible_spaces)
        space_index = relative_id % len(german_eligible_spaces)
        _, unit_type, named_unit = MOVE_UNIT_TYPES[unit_type_index]
        selected_space = german_eligible_spaces[space_index]
        if named_unit is not None:
            selected_unit = named_unit
        else:
            selected_unit = None
            for current_space in get_german_controlled_spaces():
                destination_spaces = get_adjacent_german_controlled_spaces(
                    current_space
                )
                if selected_space not in destination_spaces:
                    continue
                selected_unit = next(
                    (
                        unit
                        for unit in current_space.units
                        if isinstance(unit, GermanUnit)
                        and unit.type == unit_type
                        and can_add_unit_to_space(selected_space, unit)
                    ),
                    None
                )
                if selected_unit is not None:
                    break
            if selected_unit is None:
                return False
        do_move_unit_one_space(
            unit_choice=selected_unit,
            space_choice=selected_space
        )
        return True
    refit_panzer_start = move_unit_one_space_end
    refit_panzer_end = (
        refit_panzer_start
        + len(panzer_divisions_list)
    )
    if refit_panzer_start <= action_id < refit_panzer_end:
        panzer_index = action_id - refit_panzer_start
        selected_panzer = panzer_divisions_list[panzer_index]
        reduced_panzers = [
            panzer
            for panzer in get_panzer_divisions_in_strategic_reserve()
            if panzer.combat_value == 1
        ]
        if selected_panzer not in reduced_panzers:
            return False
        unit_choice = reduced_panzers.index(selected_panzer) + 1
        do_refit_panzer_division(
            unit_choice=unit_choice
        )
        return True
    move_ap_to_reserve_start = refit_panzer_end
    move_ap_to_reserve_end = move_ap_to_reserve_start + 1
    if move_ap_to_reserve_start <= action_id < move_ap_to_reserve_end:
        do_move_action_point_to_strategic_reserve()
        return True
    augmentation_start = move_ap_to_reserve_end
    augmentation_end = augmentation_start + 3
    if augmentation_start <= action_id < augmentation_end:
        augmentation_index = action_id - augmentation_start
        augmentation_choice = augmentation_index + 1
        return do_resource_augmentation_roll(
            choice=augmentation_choice
        )
    fortified_villages_start = augmentation_end
    fortified_villages_end = (
        fortified_villages_start
        + len(german_eligible_spaces)
    )
    if fortified_villages_start <= action_id < fortified_villages_end:
        space_index = action_id - fortified_villages_start
        selected_space = german_eligible_spaces[space_index]
        do_build_fortified_villages(
            space=selected_space
        )
        return True
    return False
def get_casualty_from_action(action_id, german_units):
    _, unit_type, named_unit, combat_value = GERMAN_LOSS_TYPES[action_id]
    if named_unit is not None:
        casualty = next(
            (
                unit
                for unit in german_units
                if (
                    unit is named_unit
                    and unit.combat_value == combat_value
                )
            ),
            None
        )
    else:
        casualty = next(
            (
                unit
                for unit in german_units
                if (
                    isinstance(unit, GermanUnit)
                    and unit.type == unit_type
                    and unit.combat_value == combat_value
                )
            ),
            None
        )
    return casualty
def select_random_casualty_ai(german_units):
    casualty_mask = get_casualty_mask(german_units)
    legal_actions = np.where(casualty_mask == 1.0)[0]
    if len(legal_actions) == 0:
        return None
    action_id = choice(legal_actions)
    return get_casualty_from_action(
        action_id,
        german_units
    )
def get_casualty_mask(german_units):
    mask = np.zeros(
        len(GERMAN_LOSS_TYPES),
        dtype=np.float32
    )
    for loss_index, (_, unit_type, named_unit, combat_value) in enumerate(
        GERMAN_LOSS_TYPES
    ):
        for unit in german_units:
            if named_unit is not None:
                unit_matches = unit is named_unit
            else:
                unit_matches = (
                    isinstance(unit, GermanUnit)
                    and unit.type == unit_type
                )
            if (
                unit_matches
                and unit.combat_value == combat_value
            ):
                mask[loss_index] = 1.0
                break
    return mask
def get_hitler_intervention_mask(card):
    mask = np.zeros(HITLER_INTERVENTION_ACTIONS, dtype=np.float32)
    targets = get_hitler_intervention_targets(card)
    if not targets:
        mask[0] = 1.0
        return mask
    stage = HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_stage
    if stage is None:
        GlobalGameState.hitler_intervention_no_effect = False
        if card.card_id == 32:
            mask[0] = 1.0
            return mask
        HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_stage = "CANCEL"
        stage = "CANCEL"
    if stage == "CANCEL":
        mask[0] = 1.0
        mask[1] = 1.0
        return mask
    if stage == "TARGET":
        if len(targets) == 1:
            HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_target = targets[0]
            HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_stage = "REDEPLOY"
            return get_hitler_intervention_mask(card)
        for index, army in enumerate(armies_list):
            if army in targets:
                mask[2 + index] = 1.0
        return mask
    if stage == "REDEPLOY":
        target_army = HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_target
        if target_army is None:
            mask[0] = 1.0
            return mask
        attacking_space = get_german_space_facing_front_line(
            target_army
        )
        current_panzer_count = sum(
            1
            for unit in attacking_space.units
            if (
                isinstance(unit, GermanUnit)
                and unit.is_panzer()
            )
        )
        if (
            HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_redeployments
            >= card.hitler_intervention_panzer_count
        ):
            mask[0] = 1.0
            return mask
        if current_panzer_count >= PANZER_STACKING_LIMIT:
            mask[0] = 1.0
            return mask
        available_panzers = create_available_panzers_list(
            attacking_space
        )
        if not available_panzers:
            mask[0] = 1.0
            return mask
        legal_units = [
            unit
            for unit, source_space in available_panzers
        ]
        for index, panzer in enumerate(panzer_divisions_list):
            if panzer in legal_units:
                mask[8 + index] = 1.0
        kampfgruppen = [
            unit for unit in legal_units
            if unit.type == ReinforcementType.KAMPFGRUPPE
        ]
        for index in range(min(len(kampfgruppen), 2)):
            mask[19 + index] = 1.0
        return mask
    mask[0] = 1.0
    return mask
def execute_flat_move_other_units_from_strategic_reserve_ai():
    while True:
        mask = get_move_other_unit_from_strategic_reserve_mask()
        legal_actions = np.where(mask == 1.0)[0]
        if len(legal_actions) == 0:
            break
        choice = randint(0, len(legal_actions))
        if choice == 0:
            break
        local_action_id = legal_actions[choice - 1]
        unit_type_index = local_action_id // len(german_eligible_spaces)
        space_index = local_action_id % len(german_eligible_spaces)
        _, unit_type, named_unit = OTHER_RESERVE_UNIT_TYPES[unit_type_index]
        selected_space = german_eligible_spaces[space_index]
        if named_unit is not None:
            selected_unit = named_unit
        else:
            selected_unit = next(
                (
                    unit
                    for unit in strategic_reserve_box.units
                    if isinstance(unit, GermanUnit)
                    and unit.type == unit_type
                ),
                None
            )
        if selected_unit is None:
            continue
        do_move_other_unit_from_strategic_reserve(
            unit_choice=selected_unit,
            space_choice=selected_space,
        )
def execute_flat_hitler_intervention_ai(action_id, card, weather):
    targets = get_hitler_intervention_targets(card)
    stage = HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_stage
    if action_id == 0:
        if not targets:
            check_hitler_intervention_applies(
                card,
                cancel_choice="N"
            )
            HeadlessDecisionState.reset_hitler_intervention()
            return False
        if stage is None and card.card_id == 32:
            target_army = check_hitler_intervention_applies(card)
            if target_army is None:
                HeadlessDecisionState.reset_hitler_intervention()
                return False
            HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_target = target_army
            HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_stage = "REDEPLOY"
            return True
        if stage == "CANCEL":
            HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_stage = "TARGET"
            return True
        if stage == "TARGET":
            if len(targets) == 1:
                HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_target = targets[0]
                HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_stage = "REDEPLOY"
            return True
        if stage == "REDEPLOY":
            target_army = HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_target
            if target_army is None:
                HeadlessDecisionState.reset_hitler_intervention()
                return False
            attacking_space = get_german_space_facing_front_line(target_army)
            do_hitler_intervention_attack(
                card,
                weather,
                target_army,
                attacking_space
            )
            HeadlessDecisionState.reset_hitler_intervention()
            return False
        return True
    if action_id == 1:
        if card.card_id == 32:
            return True
        result = check_hitler_intervention_applies(
            card,
            cancel_choice="Y",
            target_choice=1
        )
        if result is None:
            HeadlessDecisionState.reset_hitler_intervention()
            return False
        targets = get_hitler_intervention_targets(card)
        if len(targets) == 1:
            HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_target = targets[0]
            HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_stage = "REDEPLOY"
        else:
            HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_stage = "TARGET"
        return True
    if 2 <= action_id <= 7:
        selected_army = armies_list[action_id - 2]
        if selected_army not in targets:
            return True
        HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_target = selected_army
        HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_stage = "REDEPLOY"
        print(f"SELECTED TARGET: {selected_army.display_name}")
        return True
    if 8 <= action_id <= 18:
        target_army = HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_target
        if target_army is None:
            return True
        attacking_space = get_german_space_facing_front_line(target_army)
        available_panzers = create_available_panzers_list(attacking_space)
        from core.german_units import panzer_divisions_list
        selected_panzer = panzer_divisions_list[action_id - 8]
        selected_index = next(
            (
                index
                for index, (unit, source_space)
                in enumerate(available_panzers)
                if unit.name == selected_panzer.name
            ),
            None
        )
        if selected_index is None:
            return True
        do_hitler_intervention_redeploy(
            card,
            target_army,
            deployment_choices=[selected_index + 1]
        )
        HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_redeployments += 1
        return True
    if 19 <= action_id <= 20:
        target_army = HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_target
        if target_army is None:
            return True
        attacking_space = get_german_space_facing_front_line(target_army)
        available_panzers = create_available_panzers_list(attacking_space)
        kampfgruppen = [
            (unit, source_space)
            for unit, source_space in available_panzers
            if unit.type == ReinforcementType.KAMPFGRUPPE
        ]
        kampfgruppe_index = action_id - 19
        if kampfgruppe_index >= len(kampfgruppen):
            return True
        selected_unit, selected_space = kampfgruppen[kampfgruppe_index]
        selected_index = next(
            (
                index
                for index, (unit, source_space)
                in enumerate(available_panzers)
                if unit is selected_unit
                and source_space is selected_space
            ),
            None
        )
        if selected_index is None:
            return True
        do_hitler_intervention_redeploy(
            card,
            target_army,
            deployment_choices=[selected_index + 1]
        )
        HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_redeployments += 1
        return True
    return False


============================================================
PATH: core\ai\headless_action_mask_helper.py

from core.ai.headless_action_masks import (
    german_eligible_spaces,
    OTHER_RESERVE_UNIT_TYPES,
    MOVE_UNIT_TYPES,
)
from core.allied_armies import armies_list
from core.german_units import panzer_divisions_list
PASS_START = 0
COUNTER_ATTACK_START = 1
PANZER_FROM_RESERVE_START = COUNTER_ATTACK_START + len(armies_list)
PANZER_TO_RESERVE_START = PANZER_FROM_RESERVE_START + len(panzer_divisions_list) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
OTHER_FROM_RESERVE_START = PANZER_TO_RESERVE_START + len(panzer_divisions_list)
MOVE_UNIT_START = OTHER_FROM_RESERVE_START + len(OTHER_RESERVE_UNIT_TYPES) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
REFIT_PANZER_START = MOVE_UNIT_START + len(MOVE_UNIT_TYPES) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
STORE_ACTION_POINT_START = REFIT_PANZER_START + len(panzer_divisions_list)
AUGMENTATION_START = STORE_ACTION_POINT_START + 1
FORTIFIED_VILLAGE_START = AUGMENTATION_START + 3
def get_pass_action_id():
    return PASS_START
def get_counter_attack_action_id(army):
    return COUNTER_ATTACK_START + armies_list.index(army)
def get_panzer_from_reserve_action_id(panzer, destination):
    return (
        PANZER_FROM_RESERVE_START
        + panzer_divisions_list.index(panzer) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
        + german_eligible_spaces.index(destination)
    )
def get_panzer_to_reserve_action_id(panzer):
    return PANZER_TO_RESERVE_START + panzer_divisions_list.index(panzer)
def get_unit_type_index(unit, unit_types):
    for i, (_, unit_type, named_unit) in enumerate(unit_types):
        if named_unit is unit or (
            named_unit is None and unit_type == getattr(unit, "type", None)
        ):
            return i
    raise ValueError(f"Unit not found in action layout: {unit}")
def get_other_from_reserve_action_id(unit, destination):
    return (
        OTHER_FROM_RESERVE_START
        + get_unit_type_index(unit, OTHER_RESERVE_UNIT_TYPES) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
        + german_eligible_spaces.index(destination)
    )
def get_move_unit_action_id(unit, destination):
    return (
        MOVE_UNIT_START
        + get_unit_type_index(unit, MOVE_UNIT_TYPES) * len(german_eligible_spaces)
        + german_eligible_spaces.index(destination)
    )
def get_refit_panzer_action_id(panzer):
    return REFIT_PANZER_START + panzer_divisions_list.index(panzer)
def get_store_action_point_action_id():
    return STORE_ACTION_POINT_START
def get_augmentation_action_id(choice):
    if choice not in (1, 2, 3):
        raise ValueError("Augmentation choice must be 1, 2 or 3")
    return AUGMENTATION_START + choice - 1
def get_fortified_village_action_id(space):
    return FORTIFIED_VILLAGE_START + german_eligible_spaces.index(space)

PATH: core\ai\headless_env_manager.py

import io
import builtins
import os
import re
from core.global_game_state import GlobalGameState
_REAL_PRINT_ = builtins.print
_REAL_INPUT = builtins.input
_current_ai_arguments = None
_LOG_BUFFER = io.StringIO()
CAPTURE_FULL_LOG = True
def configure_headless_printing(game_number=1):
    _LOG_BUFFER.seek(0)
    _LOG_BUFFER.truncate(0)
    if CAPTURE_FULL_LOG:
        _LOG_BUFFER.write(f"=== START OF GAME {game_number} LOG ===\n\n")
        builtins.print = headless_file_logger
    else:
        builtins.print = lambda *args, **kwargs: None
def headless_file_logger(*args, **kwargs):
    if not CAPTURE_FULL_LOG:
        return
    message = " ".join(str(arg) for arg in args)
    _LOG_BUFFER.write(message + "\n")
def save_log_to_disk(game_number):
    if not CAPTURE_FULL_LOG:
        return
    log_text = _LOG_BUFFER.getvalue()
    log_text = re.sub(r"\x1b\[[0-9;]*m", "", log_text)
    os.makedirs("logs", exist_ok=True)
    with open(
        os.path.join("logs", f"game_{game_number}_log.txt"),
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8",
    ) as f:
        f.write(log_text)
def restore_normal_printing():
    builtins.print = _REAL_PRINT_
def configure_automated_inputs(auto_enter=True):
    if auto_enter:
        builtins.input = lambda *args, **kwargs: ""
    else:
        builtins.input = _REAL_INPUT
def inject_ai_turn_arguments(action_vector):
    global _current_ai_arguments
    _current_ai_arguments = action_vector
def get_automated_choice(prompt_text, ai_slot=None, default_fallback="0"):
    if getattr(GlobalGameState, "headless", False):
        if _current_ai_arguments is not None and ai_slot is not None:
            return str(_current_ai_arguments[ai_slot])
        return str(default_fallback)
    return _REAL_INPUT(prompt_text).strip()

from random import shuffle, randint
from core.actions.actions_menu import common_pre_action_phase
from core.ai.dqn.dqn_action_adapter import get_action_phase_decision, get_hitler_intervention_decision
from core.ai.headless_action_masks import execute_flat_move_other_units_from_strategic_reserve_ai
from core.allied_advances_phase import do_allied_advances_phase
from core.allied_armies import (
    BRITISH_SECOND_ARMY,
    CANADIAN_FIRST_ARMY,
    US_FIRST_ARMY,
    US_THIRD_ARMY,
    US_VIII_CORPS,
    US_XV_CORPS,
)
from core.game_constants import CYAN, GREEN, RED, RESET
from core.tables.weather import get_weather_result
from core.resources import (
    do_event,
    do_resource_phase_adjustments,
    do_resource_phase_drms,
    do_resource_phase_reinforcements,
)
from core.map.map_model import hitler_approval_track
from core.tables.carpet_bombing import get_carpet_bombing_result, ATTACK_CANCELLED
from core.global_game_state import GlobalGameState
from core.ai.headless_env_manager import (
    configure_automated_inputs,
)
from core.card_utilities import (
        calculate_attack_modifiers,
        get_all_defending_armies,
        get_armies_as_objects,
        calculate_defense_modifiers,
)
from game_phases.action_phase import common_post_action_phase
def advance_to_next_decision(draw_deck, mid_deck, late_deck):
    configure_automated_inputs(GlobalGameState.headless)
    GAME_WON = "won"
    GAME_LOST = "lost"
    GAME_CONTINUES = None
    card_28_draw_position = None
    us_3_activation_position = None
    us_3_activation_location = None
    def print_attack_strengths(card, weather, carpet_bombing):
        print(CYAN)
        print("========================================")
        print("ATTACK STRENGTHS (ALLIED ARMIES)")
        print("========================================")
        armies = get_armies_as_objects(card)
        if not armies:
            print("\t=>NO ARMIES ATTACKING")
            print(RESET)
            return
        for army in armies:
            calculate_attack_modifiers(
                card=card,
                army=army,
                num_jabos=weather.available_jabos,
                carpet_bombing=carpet_bombing,
                print_modifiers=True,
            )
        print(RESET)
    def check_for_game_end():
        if hitler_approval_track.value == -2:
            print()
            print("========================================")
            print("HITLER APPROVAL HAS FALLEN TO -2")
            print("YOU ARE RELIEVED OF COMMAND")
            print()
            print("YOU LOSE!")
            print("========================================")
            return GAME_LOST
        allied_formations = [
            US_FIRST_ARMY,
            BRITISH_SECOND_ARMY,
            CANADIAN_FIRST_ARMY,
            US_THIRD_ARMY,
            US_VIII_CORPS,
            US_XV_CORPS,
        ]
        if any(
            formation.location
            and formation.location.name == "FALAISE GAP"
            for formation in allied_formations
        ):
            print()
            print("========================================")
            print("ALLIED FORMATION HAS REACHED FALAISE GAP")
            print()
            print("YOU LOSE!")
            print("========================================")
            return GAME_LOST
        if GlobalGameState.cards_drawn == 48:
            return GAME_WON
        return GAME_CONTINUES
    def print_defense_strengths(card, weather):
        print(CYAN)
        print("========================================")
        print("DEFENSE STRENGTHS (ALLIED ARMIES)")
        print("========================================")
        if GlobalGameState.cards_drawn == 0:
            print()
            print("N/A")
            print()
        armies = get_all_defending_armies()
        for army in armies:
            calculate_defense_modifiers(
                card=card,
                army=army,
                weather=weather,
                print_modifiers=True,
            )
    while True:
        print()
        print("=========================================")
        print("NORMANDY SOLITAIRE! D-Day to Falaise 1944")
        print("=========================================")
        print()
        game_result = check_for_game_end()
        if game_result in (GAME_WON, GAME_LOST):
            break
        if GlobalGameState.drawn_cards:
            drawn_ids = [
                str(card.card_id)
                for card in GlobalGameState.drawn_cards
            ]
            print(f"List of Drawn Cards: ({', '.join(drawn_ids)})")
        else:
            print("List of Drawn Cards: []")
        print(f"Cards Drawn: {len(GlobalGameState.drawn_cards)}")
        cards_remaining = 48 - len(GlobalGameState.drawn_cards)
        print(f"Cards Remaining: {cards_remaining}")
        print(f"Cards Remaining in Deck: {len(draw_deck)}")
        print()
        if GlobalGameState.current_card:
            print(f"Current Card: {GlobalGameState.current_card.card_id}")
        if GlobalGameState.current_weather:
            print(
                f"Current Weather: "
                f"{GlobalGameState.current_weather.weather_type.value}"
            )
        print()
        menu_items = [
            "Draw Card",
            "Roll For Weather",
            "Resources Phase",
            "Deploy Non Panzer Div Reinforcements",
            "Allied Advances Phase",
            "Action Phase",
        ]
        for index, text in enumerate(menu_items, start=1):
            if GlobalGameState.current_step == index:
                print(f"{GREEN}> {text}{RESET}")
            else:
                print(f"{RED}  {text}{RESET}")
        print()
        print("Press ENTER to perform next action")
        print("Press G to see game summary")
        can_save_game = (
            GlobalGameState.current_step == 1
            and GlobalGameState.current_card is not None
        )
        can_load_game = GlobalGameState.current_step == 1
        if can_save_game:
            print("Press S to save game")
        if can_load_game:
            print("Press L to load saved game")
            print("Press Q to quit")
            print()
        if GlobalGameState.current_step == 1:
            if cards_remaining == 0:
                if GlobalGameState.cards_drawn == 48:
                    game_result = GAME_WON
                    print("DRAW DECK EXHAUSTED — GERMAN VICTORY!")
                    break
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Draw deck exhausted unexpectedly: "
                    f"cards_drawn={GlobalGameState.cards_drawn}, "
                    f"drawn_cards={len(GlobalGameState.drawn_cards)}, "
                    f"current_step={GlobalGameState.current_step}, "
                    f"remaining_deck={len(draw_deck)}"
                )   
            drawn_card = draw_deck[0]
            GlobalGameState.current_card = drawn_card
            GlobalGameState.current_weather = None
            GlobalGameState.current_carpet_bombing = 0
            draw_deck.remove(drawn_card)
            GlobalGameState.drawn_cards.append(drawn_card)
            if drawn_card.card_id == 28:
                card_28_draw_position = len(GlobalGameState.drawn_cards)
            if (
                drawn_card.card_id == 20
                and not GlobalGameState.mid_deck_added
            ):
                draw_deck.extend(mid_deck)
                shuffle(draw_deck)
                card_28 = next(card for card in draw_deck if card.card_id == 28)
                draw_deck.remove(card_28)
                draw_deck.insert(0, card_28)
                GlobalGameState.mid_deck_added = True
                print(CYAN)
                print()
                print("========================================")
                print("MID DECK ADDED")
                print("DRAW DECK SHUFFLED")
                print("========================================")
                print(RESET)
            if (
                drawn_card.card_id == 37
                and not GlobalGameState.late_deck_added
            ):
                draw_deck.extend(late_deck)
                shuffle(draw_deck)
                GlobalGameState.late_deck_added = True
                print(CYAN)
                print()
                print("========================================")
                print("LATE DECK ADDED")
                print("DRAW DECK SHUFFLED")
                print("========================================")
                print(RESET)
            print(CYAN)
            print()
            print("========================================")
            print(f"DREW CARD {drawn_card.card_id}")
            print("========================================")
            drawn_card.summary()
            print(RESET)
            input("Press ENTER to continue...")
            GlobalGameState.current_step = 2
            continue
        if GlobalGameState.current_step == 2:
            if GlobalGameState.current_card.card_id == 13:
                weather = get_weather_result(1)
                weather_roll = "N/A"
            else:
                weather_roll = randint(1, 6)
                weather = get_weather_result(weather_roll)
            GlobalGameState.current_weather = weather
            GlobalGameState.current_carpet_bombing = 0
            print(CYAN)
            print()
            print("========================================")
            print("WEATHER ROLL")
            print("========================================")
            print()
            print(f"ROLL: {weather_roll}")
            print(f"RESULT: {weather.weather_type.value}")
            if (
                GlobalGameState.current_weather.available_jabos > 0
                and GlobalGameState.current_card.air_power.has_carpet_bombing()
            ):
                print()
                print("========================================")
                print("CARPET BOMBING")
                print("========================================")
                print()
                carpet_roll = randint(1, 6)
                carpet_result = get_carpet_bombing_result(
                    die_roll=carpet_roll,
                    drm=GlobalGameState.current_weather.carpet_bombing_drm,
                )
                print(f"ROLL: {carpet_roll}")
                if GlobalGameState.current_weather.carpet_bombing_drm == 1:
                    print("DRM: +1")
                else:
                    print("DRM: 0")
                if carpet_result.attack_modifier == ATTACK_CANCELLED:
                    print("RESULT: ATTACK CANCELLED")
                    GlobalGameState.current_carpet_bombing = 0
                else:
                    GlobalGameState.current_carpet_bombing = (
                        carpet_result.attack_modifier
                    )
                    print(
                        f"RESULT: "
                        f"{GlobalGameState.current_carpet_bombing:+} "
                        f"ATTACK STRENGTH"
                    )
            print(RESET)
            input("Press ENTER to continue...")
            GlobalGameState.current_step = 3
            continue
        if GlobalGameState.current_step == 3:
            do_event(GlobalGameState.current_card)
            do_resource_phase_adjustments(
                GlobalGameState.current_card
            )
            do_resource_phase_drms(
                GlobalGameState.current_weather.weather_type,
                GlobalGameState.current_card,
            )
            print(
                f"RESOURCE PHASE - weather is "
                f"{GlobalGameState.current_weather.weather_type.value}\n"
            )
            print_attack_strengths(
                GlobalGameState.current_card,
                GlobalGameState.current_weather,
                GlobalGameState.current_carpet_bombing,
            )
            print_defense_strengths(
                GlobalGameState.current_card,
                GlobalGameState.current_weather,
            )
            print(CYAN)
            do_resource_phase_reinforcements(
                GlobalGameState.current_card
            )
            print(RESET)
            input("Press ENTER to continue...")
            GlobalGameState.current_step = 4
            continue
        if GlobalGameState.current_step == 4:
            execute_flat_move_other_units_from_strategic_reserve_ai()
            GlobalGameState.current_step = 5
            continue
        if GlobalGameState.current_step == 5:
            us_3_was_active = GlobalGameState.us_third_army_activated
            print(CYAN)
            do_allied_advances_phase(
                GlobalGameState.current_card,
                GlobalGameState.current_weather,
            )
            print(RESET)
            if (
                not us_3_was_active
                and GlobalGameState.us_third_army_activated
            ):
                us_3_activation_position = len(
                    GlobalGameState.drawn_cards
                )
                if US_FIRST_ARMY.location is not None:
                    us_3_activation_location = (
                        US_FIRST_ARMY.location.name
                    )
            game_result = check_for_game_end()
            if game_result == GAME_LOST:
                break
            GlobalGameState.current_step = 6
            proceed = common_pre_action_phase(
                GlobalGameState.current_card,
                GlobalGameState.current_weather,
            )
            if not proceed:
                observation, action_mask = get_hitler_intervention_decision(
                    GlobalGameState.current_card
                )
                return {
                    "decision_type": "hitler_intervention",
                    "observation": observation,
                    "action_mask": action_mask,
                }
            continue
        if GlobalGameState.current_step == 6:
            if (
                GlobalGameState.actions_left_this_turn == 0
                and GlobalGameState.reserve_actions == 0
            ):                
                common_post_action_phase()
                continue
            observation, action_mask = get_action_phase_decision()
            return {
                "decision_type": "action_phase",
                "observation": observation,
                "action_mask": action_mask,
            }
    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("GAME OVER")
    print()
    if game_result == GAME_WON:
        print("You won!")
    else:
        print("You lost!")
    print("========================================")
    return {
        "result": game_result,
        "card_28_draw_position": card_28_draw_position,
        "us_3_activation_position": us_3_activation_position,
        "us_3_activation_location": us_3_activation_location,
    }

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
DEBUG_PATH = Path("core/ai/dqn/logs/debug_trace.log")
def debug_pants(message):
    DEBUG_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with DEBUG_PATH.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S.%f")[:-3]
        f.write(f"{timestamp} | {message}\n")
        f.flush()


============================================================
PATH: core\ai\dqn\dqn_action_adapter.py

import numpy as np
from core.ai.dqn.dqn_game_state_encoder import (
    DecisionType,
    DecisionContext,
    encode_game_state,
)
from core.ai.headless_action_masks import (
    get_action_phase_action_mask,
    execute_flat_action_ai,
    get_casualty_mask,
    get_casualty_from_action,
    get_hitler_intervention_mask,
    execute_flat_hitler_intervention_ai,
)
def is_legal_action(action_id, mask):
    if action_id < 0 or action_id >= len(mask):
        return False
    return mask[action_id] == 1.0
def get_legal_actions(mask):
    return np.flatnonzero(mask == 1.0)
def get_action_phase_decision():
    context = DecisionContext(
        decision_type=DecisionType.ACTION_PHASE,
    )
    observation = encode_game_state(context)
    mask = get_action_phase_action_mask()
    return observation, mask
def execute_action_phase_decision(action_id):
    mask = get_action_phase_action_mask()
    if not is_legal_action(action_id, mask):
        return False
    return execute_flat_action_ai(action_id)
def get_casualty_decision(
    german_units,
    relevant_army=None,
    relevant_space=None,
    allied_attack=False,
    german_counterattack=False,
):
    context = DecisionContext(
        decision_type=DecisionType.CASUALTY,
        relevant_army=relevant_army,
        relevant_space=relevant_space,
        allied_attack=allied_attack,
        german_counterattack=german_counterattack,
    )
    observation = encode_game_state(context)
    mask = get_casualty_mask(german_units)
    return observation, mask
def execute_casualty_decision(action_id, german_units):
    mask = get_casualty_mask(german_units)
    if not is_legal_action(action_id, mask):
        return None
    return get_casualty_from_action(action_id, german_units)
def get_hitler_intervention_decision(card):
    context = DecisionContext(
        decision_type=DecisionType.HITLER_INTERVENTION,
    )
    print(
        "HITLER INTERVENTION DEBUG:",
        "card =", card,
        "targets =", card.hitler_intervention_target_armies,
    )
    mask = get_hitler_intervention_mask(card)
    observation = encode_game_state(context)
    return observation, mask
def execute_hitler_intervention_decision(action_id, card, weather):
    mask = get_hitler_intervention_mask(card)
    if not is_legal_action(action_id, mask):
        return False
    return execute_flat_hitler_intervention_ai(
        action_id,
        card,
        weather,
    )


============================================================
PATH: core\ai\dqn\dqn_agent.py
============================================================
import numpy as np
import torch
from pathlib import Path
import yaml
from core.ai.dqn.dqn_replay_buffer import DQNReplayBuffer
import copy
import torch.nn.functional as F
def load_hyperparameters(config_name="normandy_v1"):
    config_path = Path(__file__).with_name("hyperparameters.yaml")
    with config_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as file:
        configurations = yaml.safe_load(file)
    return configurations[config_name]
class DQNAgent:
    def __init__(self, policy_net, device):
        self.policy_net = policy_net
        self.device = device
        params = load_hyperparameters("normandy_v1")
        self.replay_memory_size = params["replay_memory_size"]
        self.mini_batch_size = params["mini_batch_size"]
        self.learning_rate = params["learning_rate"]
        self.discount_factor = params["discount_factor"]
        self.epsilon = params["epsilon_init"]
        self.epsilon_decay = params["epsilon_decay"]
        self.epsilon_min = params["epsilon_min"]
        self.network_sync_rate = params["network_sync_rate"]
        self.hidden_layers = params["hidden_layers"]
        self.enable_double_dqn = params["enable_double_dqn"]
        self.enable_dueling_dqn = params["enable_dueling_dqn"]
        self.replay_buffer = DQNReplayBuffer(self.replay_memory_size)
        self.target_net = copy.deepcopy(self.policy_net)
        self.target_net.to(self.device)
        self.target_net.eval()
        self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(
            self.policy_net.parameters(),
            lr=self.learning_rate,
        )
        self.training_steps = 0
        self.train_frequency = params["train_frequency"]
        if self.train_frequency < 1:
            raise ValueError("train_frequency must be at least 1")
    def select_action(self, observation, action_mask):
        legal_actions = np.flatnonzero(action_mask)
        if len(legal_actions) == 0:
            raise ValueError("No legal actions available")
        if np.random.random() < self.epsilon:
            return int(np.random.choice(legal_actions))
        with torch.no_grad():
            state = torch.as_tensor(
                observation, dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device
            ).unsqueeze(0)
            q_values = self.policy_net(state).squeeze(0)
            q_values = q_values.masked_fill(
                torch.as_tensor(action_mask, device=self.device) == 0,
                float("-inf"),
            )
            return int(torch.argmax(q_values).item())
    def train_step(self):
        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.mini_batch_size:
            return None
        (
            observations,
            actions,
            rewards,
            next_observations,
            terminated,
            next_masks,
        ) = self.replay_buffer.sample(self.mini_batch_size)
        states = torch.as_tensor(
            observations, dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device
        )
        actions = torch.as_tensor(
            actions, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device
        )
        rewards = torch.as_tensor(
            rewards, dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device
        )
        next_states = torch.as_tensor(
            next_observations, dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device
        )
        dones = torch.as_tensor(
            terminated, dtype=torch.bool, device=self.device
        )
        next_masks = torch.as_tensor(
            next_masks, dtype=torch.bool, device=self.device
        )
        current_q = self.policy_net(states).gather(
            1, actions.unsqueeze(1)
        ).squeeze(1)
        with torch.no_grad():
            valid_next = (~dones) & next_masks.any(dim=1)
            next_q = torch.zeros_like(rewards)
            if valid_next.any():
                valid_states = next_states[valid_next]
                valid_masks = next_masks[valid_next]
                if self.enable_double_dqn:
                    policy_q = self.policy_net(valid_states)
                    policy_q = policy_q.masked_fill(
                        ~valid_masks, float("-inf")
                    )
                    best_actions = policy_q.argmax(dim=1)
                    target_q = self.target_net(valid_states)
                    next_q[valid_next] = target_q.gather(
                        1, best_actions.unsqueeze(1)
                    ).squeeze(1)
                else:
                    target_q = self.target_net(valid_states)
                    target_q = target_q.masked_fill(
                        ~valid_masks, float("-inf")
                    )
                    next_q[valid_next] = target_q.max(dim=1).values
            target_values = (
                rewards + self.discount_factor * next_q
            )
        loss = F.smooth_l1_loss(current_q, target_values)
        self.optimizer.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        self.optimizer.step()
        self.training_steps += 1
        if self.training_steps % self.network_sync_rate == 0:
            self.target_net.load_state_dict(
                self.policy_net.state_dict()
            )
        return float(loss.item())
    def select_actions(self, observations, action_masks, needs_reset=None):
        num_envs = len(observations)
        if needs_reset is None:
            needs_reset = np.zeros(num_envs, dtype=bool)
        actions = np.zeros(num_envs, dtype=np.int64)
        greedy_indices = []
        greedy_observations = []
        greedy_masks = []
        for i in range(num_envs):
            if needs_reset[i]:
                continue
            mask = action_masks[i]
            legal_actions = np.flatnonzero(mask)
            if len(legal_actions) == 0:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"No legal actions available for worker {i}"
                )
            if np.random.random() < self.epsilon:
                actions[i] = int(
                    np.random.choice(legal_actions)
                )
            else:
                greedy_indices.append(i)
                greedy_observations.append(observations[i])
                greedy_masks.append(mask)
        if greedy_indices:
            with torch.no_grad():
                states = torch.as_tensor(
                    np.asarray(greedy_observations),
                    dtype=torch.float32,
                    device=self.device,
                )
                q_values = self.policy_net(states)
                masks = torch.as_tensor(
                    np.asarray(greedy_masks),
                    device=self.device,
                )
                q_values = q_values.masked_fill(
                    masks == 0,
                    float("-inf"),
                )
                selected_actions = (
                    torch.argmax(q_values, dim=1)
                    .cpu()
                    .numpy()
                )
            for index, action in zip(
                greedy_indices,
                selected_actions,
            ):
                actions[index] = int(action)
        return actions

============================================================

LOCATION_NOT_AVAILABLE = 0
LOCATION_IN_TRANSIT = 1
LOCATION_STRATEGIC_RESERVE = 2
LOCATION_ELIMINATED = 3
GERMAN_LOCATION_INDEX = {
    id(space): index + 4
    for index, space in enumerate(GERMAN_MAP_SPACES)
}
GENERIC_UNIT_BOXES = [
    in_transit_box,
    strategic_reserve_box,
    eliminated_units_box,

class DecisionType(Enum):
    ACTION_PHASE = auto()
    RESERVE_DEPLOYMENT = auto()
    CASUALTY = auto()
    HITLER_INTERVENTION = auto()
@dataclass
class DecisionContext:
    decision_type: DecisionType = None
    relevant_army: object = None
    relevant_space: object = None
    allied_attack: bool = False
    german_counterattack: bool = False
def normalize(value, minimum, maximum):
    if maximum == minimum:
        return 0.0
    normalized = (value - minimum) / (maximum - minimum)
    return float(np.clip(normalized, 0.0, 1.0))
def get_unit_location(unit):
    for space in GERMAN_MAP_SPACES:
        if unit in space.units:
            return space
    if unit in in_transit_box.units:
        return in_transit_box
    if unit in strategic_reserve_box.units:
        return strategic_reserve_box
    if unit in eliminated_units_box.units:
        return eliminated_units_box
    return None
def encode_named_unit_location(observation, start_index, unit):
    location = get_unit_location(unit)
    if location is None:
        location_index = LOCATION_NOT_AVAILABLE
    elif location is in_transit_box:
        location_index = LOCATION_IN_TRANSIT
    elif location is strategic_reserve_box:
        location_index = LOCATION_STRATEGIC_RESERVE
    elif location is eliminated_units_box:
        location_index = LOCATION_ELIMINATED
    else:
        location_index = GERMAN_LOCATION_INDEX.get(id(location))
        if location_index is None:
            raise ValueError(
                f"German unit {unit.name} is in unsupported "
                f"location {location.name}"
            )
    observation[start_index + location_index] = 1.0
def count_generic_units(space, unit_type, combat_value=None):
    count = 0
    for unit in space.units:
        if not isinstance(unit, GermanUnit):
            continue
        if unit.type != unit_type:
            continue
        if combat_value is not None and unit.combat_value != combat_value:
            continue
        count += 1
    return count
def get_allied_track(army):
    if army is US_FIRST_ARMY:
        return us_1_track
    if army is BRITISH_SECOND_ARMY:
        return brit_2_track
    if army is CANADIAN_FIRST_ARMY:
        return can_1_track
    if army is US_THIRD_ARMY:
        return us_xv_track
    if army is US_VIII_CORPS:
        return us_viii_track
    if army is US_XV_CORPS:
        return us_xv_track
    raise ValueError(
        f"Unknown Allied army: {army}"
    )
def get_allied_progress(army):
    if army.location is None:
        return 0.0
    track = get_allied_track(army)
    try:
        current_index = track.index(army.location)
    except ValueError:
        return 0.0
    if len(track) <= 1:
        return 0.0
    return current_index / (len(track) - 1)
def army_was_counterattacked(army_name):
    counterattacked = GlobalGameState.counter_attacked_armies
    return army_name in counterattacked
def encode_campaign_state(observation):
    observation[CARDS_DRAWN_INDEX] = (
        GlobalGameState.cards_drawn / MAX_CARDS
    )
    current_card = GlobalGameState.current_card
    if current_card is not None:
        card_id = current_card.card_id
        if 1 <= card_id <= MAX_CARDS:
            observation[
                CURRENT_CARD_START + card_id - 1
            ] = 1.0
    for card in GlobalGameState.drawn_cards:
        if hasattr(card, "card_id"):
            card_id = card.card_id
        else:
            card_id = int(card)
        if 1 <= card_id <= MAX_CARDS:
            observation[
                DRAWN_CARDS_START + card_id - 1
            ] = 1.0
    observation[MID_DECK_ADDED_INDEX] = float(
        GlobalGameState.mid_deck_added
    )
    observation[LATE_DECK_ADDED_INDEX] = float(
        GlobalGameState.late_deck_added
    )
def encode_weather(observation):
    weather = GlobalGameState.current_weather
    if weather is None:
        return
    if weather.weather_type == WeatherType.CLEAR:
        observation[WEATHER_CLEAR_INDEX] = 1.0
    elif weather.weather_type == WeatherType.PARTLY_CLEAR:
        observation[WEATHER_PARTLY_CLEAR_INDEX] = 1.0
    elif weather.weather_type == WeatherType.OVERCAST:
        observation[WEATHER_OVERCAST_INDEX] = 1.0
def encode_resources(observation):
    observation[TRANSPORT_INDEX] = (
        transport_track.value / MAX_TRANSPORT
    )
    observation[SUPPLY_INDEX] = (
        supply_track.value / MAX_SUPPLY
    )
    observation[HITLER_APPROVAL_INDEX] = normalize(
        hitler_approval_track.value,
        MIN_HITLER_APPROVAL,
        MAX_HITLER_APPROVAL,
    )
    observation[TRANSPORT_BASE_INDEX] = (
        GlobalGameState.transport_base_level
        / MAX_TRANSPORT
    )
    observation[SUPPLY_BASE_INDEX] = (
        GlobalGameState.supply_base_level
        / MAX_SUPPLY
    )
    observation[HITLER_APPROVAL_BASE_INDEX] = normalize(
        GlobalGameState.hitler_approval_base_level,
        MIN_HITLER_APPROVAL,
        MAX_HITLER_APPROVAL,
    )
    observation[ACTIONS_LEFT_INDEX] = normalize(
        GlobalGameState.actions_left_this_turn,
        0,
        MAX_ACTIONS,
    )
    observation[RESERVE_ACTIONS_INDEX] = normalize(
        GlobalGameState.reserve_actions,
        0,
        MAX_RESERVE_ACTIONS,
    )
def encode_allied_armies(observation):
    index = ALLIED_ARMIES_START
    for army in ALLIED_ARMIES:
        observation[index] = get_allied_progress(army)
        observation[index + 1] = float(
            army.location is not None
        )
        observation[index + 2] = float(
            army.flipped
        )
        observation[index + 3] = float(
            army_was_counterattacked(army.name)
        )
        index += ALLIED_FEATURES_PER_ARMY
    observation[US_THIRD_ACTIVATED_INDEX] = float(
        GlobalGameState.us_third_army_activated
    )
    observation[US_THIRD_MERGED_INDEX] = float(
        GlobalGameState.us_third_army_merged
    )
    observation[ARMIES_UPGRADED_INDEX] = float(
        GlobalGameState.armies_upgraded
    )
def encode_panzer_divisions(observation):
    index = PANZERS_START
    for unit in panzer_divisions_list:
        encode_named_unit_location(
            observation,
            index,
            unit,
        )
        observation[index + 31] = float(
            unit.combat_value == 1
        )
        index += PANZER_FEATURES_PER_UNIT
def encode_special_german_units(observation):
    encode_named_unit_location(
        observation,
        FS3_START,
        FS_3,
    )
    observation[FS3_REDUCED_INDEX] = float(
        FS_3.combat_value == 1
    )
    encode_named_unit_location(
        observation,
        FS5_START,
        FS_5,
    )
    encode_named_unit_location(
        observation,
        TIGER_101_START,
        TIGER_101,
    )
def encode_commanders(observation):
    encode_named_unit_location(
        observation,
        ROMMEL_START,
        ROMMEL,
    )
    encode_named_unit_location(
        observation,
        MEYER_START,
        MEYER,
    )
    encode_named_unit_location(
        observation,
        MODEL_START,
        MODEL,
    )
    observation[MEYER_AVAILABLE_INDEX] = float(
        GlobalGameState.meyer_available
    )
    observation[MODEL_IN_COMMAND_INDEX] = float(
        GlobalGameState.model_in_command
    )
def encode_generic_map_units(observation):
    index = GENERIC_MAP_UNITS_START
    for space in GERMAN_MAP_SPACES:
        full_flak = count_generic_units(
            space,
            ReinforcementType.FLAK_88,
            combat_value=2,
        )
        reduced_flak = count_generic_units(
            space,
            ReinforcementType.FLAK_88,
            combat_value=1,
        )
        kampfgruppen = count_generic_units(
            space,
            ReinforcementType.KAMPFGRUPPE,
        )
        nebelwerfers = count_generic_units(
            space,
            ReinforcementType.NEBELWERFER,
        )
        observation[index] = normalize(
            full_flak,
            0,
            MAX_FLAK_STACK,
        )
        observation[index + 1] = normalize(
            reduced_flak,
            0,
            MAX_FLAK_STACK,
        )
        observation[index + 2] = normalize(
            kampfgruppen,
            0,
            MAX_KAMPFGRUPPE_STACK,
        )
        observation[index + 3] = normalize(
            nebelwerfers,
            0,
            MAX_NEBELWERFER_STACK,
        )
        index += GENERIC_FEATURES_PER_SPACE
def encode_generic_box_units(observation):
    index = GENERIC_BOX_UNITS_START
    for box in GENERIC_UNIT_BOXES:
        full_flak = count_generic_units(
            box,
            ReinforcementType.FLAK_88,
            combat_value=2,
        )
        reduced_flak = count_generic_units(
            box,
            ReinforcementType.FLAK_88,
            combat_value=1,
        )
        kampfgruppen = count_generic_units(
            box,
            ReinforcementType.KAMPFGRUPPE,
        )
        nebelwerfers = count_generic_units(
            box,
            ReinforcementType.NEBELWERFER,
        )
        observation[index] = normalize(
            full_flak,
            0,
            MAX_TOTAL_FLAK,
        )
        observation[index + 1] = normalize(
            reduced_flak,
            0,
            MAX_TOTAL_FLAK,
        )
        observation[index + 2] = normalize(
            kampfgruppen,
            0,
            MAX_TOTAL_KAMPFGRUPPE,
        )
        observation[index + 3] = normalize(
            nebelwerfers,
            0,
            MAX_TOTAL_NEBELWERFER,
        )
        index += GENERIC_FEATURES_PER_BOX
def encode_map_state(observation):
    index = MAP_STATE_START
    for space in GERMAN_MAP_SPACES:
        observation[index] = float(
            space.controlling_player == SideType.GERMAN
        )
        observation[index + 1] = float(
            space.under_siege
        )
        observation[index + 2] = normalize(
            space.fortified_village_modifier,
            0,
            MAX_FORTIFIED_VILLAGE_MODIFIER,
        )
        observation[index + 3] = 0.0
        index += MAP_FEATURES_PER_SPACE
def encode_global_game_state(observation):
    observation[CHERBOURG_CAPTURED_INDEX] = float(
        GlobalGameState.cherbourg_captured
    )
    observation[HITLER_ASSASSINATION_INDEX] = float(
        GlobalGameState.hitler_assassination
    )
    observation[HITLER_INTERVENTION_NO_EFFECT_INDEX] = float(
        GlobalGameState.hitler_intervention_no_effect
    )
    observation[BOCAGE_DEFENSE_INDEX] = normalize(
        GlobalGameState.bocage_defense_modifier,
        MIN_BOCAGE_DEFENSE_MODIFIER,
        MAX_BOCAGE_DEFENSE_MODIFIER,
    )
    observation[US_FIRST_FURTHEST_ADVANCE_INDEX] = normalize(
        11 - GlobalGameState.us_first_army_furthest_advance,
        0,
        11,
    )
    observation[CURRENT_CARPET_BOMBING_INDEX] = normalize(
        GlobalGameState.current_carpet_bombing,
        MIN_CARPET_BOMBING,
        MAX_CARPET_BOMBING,
    )
def encode_drms(observation):
    observation[TRANSPORT_ROLL_DRM_INDEX] = normalize(
        GlobalGameState.transport_roll_drm,
        MIN_TRANSPORT_ROLL_DRM,
        MAX_TRANSPORT_ROLL_DRM,
    )
    observation[SUPPLY_ROLL_DRM_INDEX] = normalize(
        GlobalGameState.supply_roll_drm,
        MIN_SUPPLY_ROLL_DRM,
        MAX_SUPPLY_ROLL_DRM,
    )
    observation[TRANSPORT_CHECK_DRM_INDEX] = normalize(
        GlobalGameState.transport_check_drm,
        MIN_TRANSPORT_CHECK_DRM,
        MAX_TRANSPORT_CHECK_DRM,
    )
    observation[SUPPLY_CHECK_DRM_INDEX] = normalize(
        GlobalGameState.supply_check_drm,
        MIN_SUPPLY_CHECK_DRM,
        MAX_SUPPLY_CHECK_DRM,
    )
    observation[HITLER_APPROVAL_CHECK_DRM_INDEX] = normalize(
        GlobalGameState.hitler_approval_check_drm,
        MIN_HITLER_APPROVAL_CHECK_DRM,
        MAX_HITLER_APPROVAL_CHECK_DRM,
    )
def encode_decision_type(observation, context):
    if context is None:
        return
    if context.decision_type is None:
        return
    if context.decision_type == DecisionType.ACTION_PHASE:
        observation[DECISION_ACTION_PHASE_INDEX] = 1.0
    elif context.decision_type == DecisionType.RESERVE_DEPLOYMENT:
        observation[DECISION_RESERVE_DEPLOYMENT_INDEX] = 1.0
    elif context.decision_type == DecisionType.CASUALTY:
        observation[DECISION_CASUALTY_INDEX] = 1.0
    elif context.decision_type == DecisionType.HITLER_INTERVENTION:
        observation[DECISION_HITLER_INTERVENTION_INDEX] = 1.0
def encode_decision_context(observation, context):
    if context is None:
        return
    if context.relevant_army is not None:
        try:
            army_index = ALLIED_ARMIES.index(
                context.relevant_army
            )
            observation[
                DECISION_ARMY_START + army_index
            ] = 1.0
        except ValueError:
            pass
    if context.relevant_space is not None:
        try:
            space_index = GERMAN_MAP_SPACES.index(
                context.relevant_space
            )
            observation[
                DECISION_SPACE_START + space_index
            ] = 1.0
        except ValueError:
            pass
    observation[DECISION_ALLIED_ATTACK_INDEX] = float(
        context.allied_attack
    )
    observation[DECISION_GERMAN_COUNTERATTACK_INDEX] = float(
        context.german_counterattack
    )
def encode_hitler_intervention_state(observation):
    stage = HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_stage
    if stage is None:
        observation[HITLER_STAGE_NONE_INDEX] = 1.0
    elif stage == "CANCEL":
        observation[HITLER_STAGE_CANCEL_INDEX] = 1.0
    elif stage == "TARGET":
        observation[HITLER_STAGE_TARGET_INDEX] = 1.0
    elif stage == "REDEPLOY":
        observation[HITLER_STAGE_REDEPLOY_INDEX] = 1.0
    else:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Unknown Hitler Intervention stage: {stage}"
        )
    target = HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_target
    if target is not None:
        try:
            target_index = ALLIED_ARMIES.index(target)
            observation[
                HITLER_TARGET_START + target_index
            ] = 1.0
        except ValueError:
            pass
    observation[HITLER_REDEPLOYMENTS_INDEX] = normalize(
        HeadlessDecisionState.hitler_intervention_redeployments,
        0,
        2,
    )
def encode_game_state(context=None):
    observation = np.zeros(
        OBSERVATION_SIZE,
        dtype=np.float32,
    )
    encode_campaign_state(observation)
    encode_weather(observation)
    encode_resources(observation)
    encode_allied_armies(observation)
    encode_panzer_divisions(observation)
    encode_special_german_units(observation)
    encode_commanders(observation)
    encode_generic_map_units(observation)
    encode_generic_box_units(observation)
    encode_map_state(observation)
    encode_global_game_state(observation)
    encode_drms(observation)
    encode_decision_type(
        observation,
        context,
    )
    encode_decision_context(
        observation,
        context,
    )
    encode_hitler_intervention_state(
        observation
    )
    validate_observation(observation)
    return observation
def validate_observation(observation):
    if observation.shape != (OBSERVATION_SIZE,):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Observation has shape {observation.shape}; "
            f"expected ({OBSERVATION_SIZE},)"
        )
    if observation.dtype != np.float32:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Observation dtype is {observation.dtype}; "
            f"expected float32"
        )
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(observation)):
        raise ValueError(
            "Observation contains NaN or infinite values"
        )
    if np.any(observation < 0.0):
        bad_indices = np.where(
            observation < 0.0
        )[0]
        raise ValueError(
            f"Observation contains values below 0 "
            f"at indices {bad_indices.tolist()}"
        )
    if np.any(observation > 1.0):
        bad_indices = np.where(
            observation > 1.0
        )[0]
        raise ValueError(
            f"Observation contains values above 1 "
            f"at indices {bad_indices.tolist()}"
        )
def get_active_observation_indices(observation):
    return np.where(
        observation != 0.0
    )[0].tolist()
def print_observation_summary(observation):
    print()
    print("========================================")
    print("DQN OBSERVATION")
    print("========================================")
    print(f"Shape: {observation.shape}")
    print(f"Dtype: {observation.dtype}")
    print(f"Minimum: {observation.min():.3f}")
    print(f"Maximum: {observation.max():.3f}")
    print(
        f"Non-zero features: "
        f"{np.count_nonzero(observation)}"
    )
    print("========================================")

PATH: core\ai\dqn\dqn_network.py
