from torch import nn
from core.ai.dqn.dqn_agent import load_hyperparameters
class DQNNetwork(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, observation_size, action_size):
        super().__init__()
        params = load_hyperparameters()
        hidden_layers = params["hidden_layers"]
        layers = []
        input_size = observation_size
        for hidden_size in hidden_layers:
            layers.append(nn.Linear(input_size, hidden_size))
            layers.append(nn.ReLU())
            input_size = hidden_size
        layers.append(nn.Linear(input_size, action_size))
        self.network = nn.Sequential(*layers)
        print(self.network)
    def forward(self, observation):
        return self.network(observation)


============================================================
PATH: core\ai\dqn\dqn_replay_buffer.py
============================================================
import random
from collections import deque
import numpy as np
class DQNReplayBuffer:
    def __init__(self, capacity):
        self.buffer = deque(maxlen=capacity)
    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.buffer)
    def add(
        self,
        observation,
        action_id,
        reward,
        next_observation,
        terminated,
        next_action_mask,
    ):
        experience = (
            np.array(observation, dtype=np.float32, copy=True),
            int(action_id),
            float(reward),
            np.array(next_observation, dtype=np.float32, copy=True),
            bool(terminated),
            np.array(next_action_mask, dtype=np.float32, copy=True),
        )
        self.buffer.append(experience)
    def sample(self, batch_size):
        experiences = random.sample(self.buffer, batch_size)
        observations, actions, rewards, next_observations, terminated, next_masks = zip(
            *experiences
        )
        return (
            np.stack(observations),
            np.array(actions, dtype=np.int64),
            np.array(rewards, dtype=np.float32),
            np.stack(next_observations),
            np.array(terminated, dtype=np.bool_),
            np.stack(next_masks),
        )


============================================================
PATH: core\ai\dqn\dqn_save_load.py
============================================================
from __future__ import annotations
import base64
import copy
import json
import pickle
import random
from enum import Enum
from pathlib import Path
from cards import decks
from core.global_game_state import GlobalGameState
from core.ai.headless_action_masks import HeadlessDecisionState
from core import german_units, allied_armies
from core.map.map_model import (
    transport_track, supply_track, hitler_approval_track,
    in_transit_box, strategic_reserve_box, eliminated_units_box,
)
from core.map.map_spaces_us_1 import us_1_track
from core.map.map_spaces_brit_2 import brit_2_track
from core.map.map_spaces_can_1 import can_1_track
from core.map.map_spaces_us_3 import us_viii_track, us_xv_track
from core.models import GermanUnit, AlliedArmy
from core.tables.weather import WeatherResult, WeatherType
from core.models import Strategy
from core.enums import SideType
VERSION = 1
DEFAULT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / 'saved_games'
CARDS = {c.card_id: c for c in decks.draw_deck + decks.mid_deck + decks.late_deck}
def _spaces():
    result = {}
    for track in (us_1_track, brit_2_track, can_1_track, us_viii_track, us_xv_track):
        for space in track:
            if space.name in result and result[space.name] is not space:
                raise ValueError(f'Duplicate map-space name: {space.name}')
            result[space.name] = space
    return result
def _named_units():
    result = {}
    for module in (german_units, allied_armies):
        for key, obj in vars(module).items():
            if isinstance(obj, (GermanUnit, AlliedArmy)):
                result[id(obj)] = (f'{module.__name__}.{key}', obj)
    return {name: obj for name, obj in result.values()}
def _pack_rng():
    return base64.b64encode(pickle.dumps(random.getstate(), protocol=4)).decode('ascii')
def _unpack_rng(value):
    return pickle.loads(base64.b64decode(value.encode('ascii')))
def capture_state(env, *, metadata=None):
    spaces = _spaces()
    boxes = {'in_transit': in_transit_box, 'strategic_reserve': strategic_reserve_box,
             'eliminated_units': eliminated_units_box}
    named = _named_units()
    objects = {}
    id_to_key = {}
    for name, obj in named.items():
        id_to_key[id(obj)] = name
    next_dynamic = 0
    def unit_key(unit):
        nonlocal next_dynamic
        identity = id(unit)
        if identity not in id_to_key:
            id_to_key[identity] = f'dynamic:{next_dynamic}'
            next_dynamic += 1
        key = id_to_key[identity]
        if key not in objects:
            if isinstance(unit, GermanUnit):
                objects[key] = {'kind': 'german', 'name': unit.name,
                                'type': unit.type.name, 'combat_value': unit.combat_value}
            elif isinstance(unit, AlliedArmy):
                objects[key] = {'kind': 'allied', 'name': unit.name,
                                'strength': unit._strength, 'reverse_strength': unit.reverse_strength,
                                'flipped': unit.flipped, 'merged': unit.merged,
                                'location': unit.location.name if unit.location else None}
            else:
                raise TypeError(f'Unexpected unit: {unit!r}')
        return key
    def encode(value):
        if value is None or isinstance(value, (bool, int, float, str)):
            return value
        if isinstance(value, Enum):
            return {'$enum': [type(value).__module__, type(value).__name__, value.name]}
        if isinstance(value, (GermanUnit, AlliedArmy)):
            return {'$unit': unit_key(value)}
        if hasattr(value, 'card_id'):
            return {'$card': value.card_id}
        if isinstance(value, WeatherResult):
            return {'$weather': {'weather_type': value.weather_type.name,
                                 'available_jabos': value.available_jabos,
                                 'resource_drm': value.resource_drm,
                                 'carpet_bombing_drm': value.carpet_bombing_drm}}
        if hasattr(value, 'name') and value.name in spaces and spaces[value.name] is value:
            return {'$space': value.name}
        if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
            return [encode(x) for x in value]
        if isinstance(value, set):
            return {'$set': [encode(x) for x in value]}
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {'$dict': [[encode(k), encode(v)] for k, v in value.items()]}
        raise TypeError(f'Cannot serialize {type(value).__name__}: {value!r}')
    global_values = {k: encode(v) for k, v in vars(GlobalGameState).items()
                     if not k.startswith('_') and not isinstance(v, (classmethod, staticmethod, property)) and not callable(v)}
    decision_values = {
        key: encode(getattr(HeadlessDecisionState, key))
        for key in (
            'hitler_intervention_stage',
            'hitler_intervention_target',
            'hitler_intervention_redeployments',
        )
    }
    space_values = {name: {'controlling_player': space.controlling_player.name,
                           'fortified_village_modifier': space.fortified_village_modifier,
                           'fortified': space.fortified,
                           'model_modifier': space.model_modifier,
                           'under_siege': space.under_siege,
                           'units': [unit_key(u) for u in space.units]}
                    for name, space in spaces.items()}
    box_values = {name: [unit_key(u) for u in box.units] for name, box in boxes.items()}
    for unit in named.values():
        unit_key(unit)
    result = {
        'version': VERSION,
        'metadata': copy.deepcopy(metadata or {}),
        'global_game_state': global_values,
        'decision_state': decision_values,
        'current_decision_type': env.current_decision_type,
        'decks': {'draw': [c.card_id for c in env.draw_deck],
                  'mid': [c.card_id for c in env.mid_deck],
                  'late': [c.card_id for c in env.late_deck]},
        'resources': {'transport': transport_track.value, 'supply': supply_track.value,
                      'hitler_approval': hitler_approval_track.value},
        'spaces': space_values,
        'boxes': box_values,
        'units': objects,
        'python_rng': _pack_rng(),
    }
    json.dumps(result)
    return result
def restore_state(env, snapshot):
    if snapshot.get('version') != VERSION:
        raise ValueError(f'Unsupported DQN snapshot version: {snapshot.get("version")}')
    spaces = _spaces()
    named = _named_units()
    units = {}
    for key, data in snapshot['units'].items():
        if key in named:
            unit = named[key]
        elif key.startswith('dynamic:') and data['kind'] == 'german':
            from core.enums import ReinforcementType
            unit = GermanUnit(type=ReinforcementType[data['type']], name=data['name'],
                              combat_value=data['combat_value'])
        else:
            raise ValueError(f'Unknown saved unit identifier: {key}')
        units[key] = unit
    def decode(value):
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [decode(x) for x in value]
        if not isinstance(value, dict):
            return value
        if '$unit' in value:
            return units[value['$unit']]
        if '$card' in value:
            return CARDS[value['$card']]
        if '$space' in value:
            return spaces[value['$space']]
        if '$set' in value:
            return set(decode(x) for x in value['$set'])
        if '$dict' in value:
            return {decode(k): decode(v) for k, v in value['$dict']}
        if '$weather' in value:
            v = value['$weather']
            return WeatherResult(weather_type=WeatherType[v['weather_type']],
                                 available_jabos=v['available_jabos'],
                                 resource_drm=v['resource_drm'],
                                 carpet_bombing_drm=v['carpet_bombing_drm'])
        if '$enum' in value:
            import importlib
            module, cls, member = value['$enum']
            if not module.startswith('core.'):
                raise ValueError(f'Unexpected enum module: {module}')
            return getattr(importlib.import_module(module), cls)[member]
        return {k: decode(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    for name, data in snapshot['units'].items():
        unit = units[name]
        if data['kind'] == 'german':
            from core.enums import ReinforcementType
            unit.type = ReinforcementType[data['type']]
            unit.name = data['name']
            unit.combat_value = data['combat_value']
        else:
            unit._strength = data['strength']
            unit.reverse_strength = data['reverse_strength']
            unit.flipped = data['flipped']
            unit.merged = data['merged']
            unit.location = spaces[data['location']] if data['location'] else None
    for name, value in snapshot['global_game_state'].items():
        setattr(GlobalGameState, name, decode(value))
    for name, value in snapshot['decision_state'].items():
        setattr(HeadlessDecisionState, name, decode(value))
    for key, track in (('transport', transport_track), ('supply', supply_track),
                       ('hitler_approval', hitler_approval_track)):
        track.value = snapshot['resources'][key]
    for name, data in snapshot['spaces'].items():
        space = spaces[name]
        space.controlling_player = SideType[data['controlling_player']]
        space.fortified_village_modifier = data['fortified_village_modifier']
        space.fortified = data['fortified']
        space.model_modifier = data['model_modifier']
        space.under_siege = data['under_siege']
        space.units[:] = [units[key] for key in data['units']]
    for name, box in (('in_transit', in_transit_box), ('strategic_reserve', strategic_reserve_box),
                      ('eliminated_units', eliminated_units_box)):
        box.units[:] = [units[key] for key in snapshot['boxes'][name]]
    env.draw_deck = [CARDS[c] for c in snapshot['decks']['draw']]
    env.mid_deck = [CARDS[c] for c in snapshot['decks']['mid']]
    env.late_deck = [CARDS[c] for c in snapshot['decks']['late']]
    env.current_decision_type = snapshot['current_decision_type']
    random.setstate(_unpack_rng(snapshot['python_rng']))
    return snapshot.get('metadata', {})
def save_game(env, path, *, metadata=None):
    snapshot = capture_state(env, metadata=metadata)
    save_snapshot(snapshot, path)
    return Path(path)
def save_snapshot(snapshot, path):
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json.dump(snapshot, f, separators=(',', ':'))
    return path
def load_snapshot(path):
    with Path(path).open('r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        snapshot = json.load(f)
    if snapshot.get('version') != VERSION:
        raise ValueError('Incompatible snapshot format')
    return snapshot
def load_game(env, path):
    return restore_state(env, load_snapshot(path))
def _midgame_config(config=None):
    if config is None:
        from core.ai.dqn.midgame_config import MIDGAME_CONFIG
        config = MIDGAME_CONFIG
    return config
def _midgame_library(config):
    return Path(config['library_path'])
class MidgameCollector:
    def __init__(self, config=None):
        self.config = _midgame_config(config)
        self.pending = {}
        self.source_game_id = None
    def start_game(self, source_game_id):
        self.source_game_id = str(source_game_id)
        self.pending.clear()
    def consider(self, env):
        if not self.config.get('collect_states', False):
            return False
        if self.source_game_id is None:
            raise RuntimeError('Call start_game(source_game_id) first')
        turn = int(GlobalGameState.cards_drawn)
        if turn not in self.config['start_turns'] or turn in self.pending:
            return False
        if env.current_decision_type not in ('action_phase', 'hitler_intervention'):
            return False
        self.pending[turn] = capture_state(env, metadata={
            'source_game_id': self.source_game_id,
            'start_turn': turn,
        })
        return True
    def finish(self, final_turn, result, *, source_checkpoint=None, original_epsilon=None):
        if self.source_game_id is None:
            raise RuntimeError('Call start_game(source_game_id) first')
        library = _midgame_library(self.config)
        saved = []
        for turn, snapshot in sorted(self.pending.items()):
            meta = snapshot['metadata']
            meta['original_final_turn'] = int(final_turn)
            meta['original_result'] = str(result)
            if source_checkpoint is not None:
                meta['source_checkpoint'] = str(source_checkpoint)
            if original_epsilon is not None:
                meta['original_epsilon'] = float(original_epsilon)
            safe_id = ''.join(c if c.isalnum() or c in '-_' else '_'
                              for c in self.source_game_id)
            path = (
                library / f'turn_{turn:02d}'
                / f'game_start{turn:02d}_end{int(final_turn):02d}_{result}_{safe_id[:8]}.json'
            )
            if path.exists():
                raise FileExistsError(f'Midgame snapshot already exists: {path}')
            saved.append(save_snapshot(snapshot, path))
        self.pending.clear()
        self.source_game_id = None
        return saved
def choose_midgame_snapshot(config=None, *, rng=None):
    config = _midgame_config(config)
    rng = rng or random
    turns = config['start_turns']
    weights = config['turn_weights']
    if len(turns) != len(weights) or not turns:
        raise ValueError('start_turns and turn_weights must be nonempty and aligned')
    if config.get('selection', 'random') != 'random':
        raise ValueError('Only random midgame selection is supported')
    if any(float(w) < 0 for w in weights):
        raise ValueError('turn_weights cannot be negative')
    candidates = []
    for turn, weight in zip(turns, weights):
        paths = sorted((_midgame_library(config) / f'turn_{int(turn):02d}').glob('*.json'))
        if paths and weight > 0:
            candidates.append((int(turn), float(weight), paths))
    if not candidates:
        raise FileNotFoundError('No eligible midgame snapshots in the configured library')
    selected = rng.choices(candidates, weights=[x[1] for x in candidates], k=1)[0]
    path = rng.choice(selected[2])
    snapshot = load_snapshot(path)
    if int(snapshot['metadata']['start_turn']) != selected[0]:
        raise ValueError(f'Snapshot turn metadata does not match folder: {path}')
    return snapshot, path
def restore_random_midgame(env, config=None, *, rng=None):
    snapshot, path = choose_midgame_snapshot(config, rng=rng)
    metadata = restore_state(env, snapshot)
    return metadata, path

import csv
from pathlib import Path
import os
import contextlib
from time import perf_counter
from tkinter.font import NORMAL
from core.ai.dqn.normandy_env import NormandyEnv
from core.ai.dqn.dqn_network import DQNNetwork
from core.ai.dqn.dqn_agent import DQNAgent
from core.ai.dqn.debug import debug_pants
import numpy as np
import torch
from gymnasium.vector import AsyncVectorEnv, AutoresetMode
import multiprocessing
import sys
import pickle
from functools import partial
from core.ai.dqn.dqn_agent import load_hyperparameters
from core.ai.dqn.normandy_logging_env import NormandyLoggingEnv
import json
from core.ai.dqn.midgame_config import MIDGAME_CONFIG
def make_silent_normandy_env(
    game_config,
    error_config,
    shared_count,
    shared_lock,
):
    if multiprocessing.current_process().name != "MainProcess":
        sys.stdout = open(os.devnull, "w", encoding="utf-8")
        sys.stderr = open(os.devnull, "w", encoding="utf-8")
    return NormandyLoggingEnv(
        config=game_config,
        shared_count=shared_count,
        shared_lock=shared_lock,
        error_config=error_config,
    )
class DQNTrainer:
    def __init__(self, env=None, agent=None):
        self.env = env if env is not None else NormandyEnv()
        if agent is None:
            observation_size = self.env.observation_space.shape[0]
            action_size = self.env.action_space.n
            device = torch.device(
                "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
            )
            network = DQNNetwork(
                observation_size,
                action_size,
            ).to(device)
            agent = DQNAgent(network, device)
        self.agent = agent
        self.games_completed = 0
        self.total_decisions = 0
        self.total_training_updates = 0
        self._evaluation_pool = None
    def evaluate_policy(self):
        if self._evaluation_pool is None:
            self._evaluation_pool = multiprocessing.get_context("spawn").Pool(
                processes=1
            )
    def close_evaluation_pool(self, force=False):
        pool = self._evaluation_pool
        self._evaluation_pool = None
        if pool is None:
            return
        if force:
            pool.terminate()
        else:
            pool.close()
        pool.join()
    def log_evaluation(
        self,
        training_games,
        result,
        path="core/ai/dqn/logs/evaluation_history.csv",
    ):
        path = Path(path)
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        new_file = not path.exists() or path.stat().st_size == 0
        with path.open("a", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            writer = csv.writer(file)
            if new_file:
                writer.writerow([
                    "training_games",
                    "epsilon",
                    "turn_reached",
                    "result",
                ])
            writer.writerow([
                training_games,
                0.0,
                result["turn_reached"],
                result["result"],
            ])
    def run_silently(self, function, *args, **kwargs):
        with open(os.devnull, "w") as null_output, contextlib.redirect_stdout(null_output):
            return function(*args, **kwargs)
    def print_checkpoint_performance(
        self,
        results,
        progress_interval,
        run_start,
        decisions_before,
        updates_before,
        num_workers,
    ):
        if not results:
            return
        elapsed = perf_counter() - run_start
        n = len(results)
        turns = np.array(
            [r["turn_reached"] for r in results],
            dtype=np.int32,
        )
        wins = sum(r["result"] == "won" for r in results)
        decisions = self.total_decisions - decisions_before
        updates = self.total_training_updates - updates_before
        def batch_stats(batch):
            values = np.array(
                [r["turn_reached"] for r in batch],
                dtype=np.int32,
            )
            return {
                "avg": float(np.mean(values)),
                "p20": 100 * float(np.mean(values >= 20)),
                "p30": 100 * float(np.mean(values >= 30)),
                "p40": 100 * float(np.mean(values >= 40)),
            }
        complete_batches = n // progress_interval
        print()
        print("=" * 70)
        print("DQN CHECKPOINT PERFORMANCE")
        print("=" * 70)
        print(f"Cumulative games:       {self.games_completed:,}")
        print(f"Games this run:         {n:,}")
        print(f"Parallel workers:       {num_workers}")
        print(f"Train frequency:        {self.agent.train_frequency}")
        print(f"Games won this run:     {wins}")
        print(f"Win rate:               {100 * wins / n:.3f}%")
        print(f"Average turn reached:   {np.mean(turns):.2f}")
        print(f"Reached turn 20+:       {100 * np.mean(turns >= 20):.1f}%")
        print(f"Reached turn 30+:       {100 * np.mean(turns >= 30):.1f}%")
        print(f"Reached turn 40+:       {100 * np.mean(turns >= 40):.1f}%")
        if MIDGAME_CONFIG["enabled"]:
            original_end = self.previous_end_turn
            print(
                f"Exceeded original end ({original_end}): "
                f"{100 * np.mean(turns > original_end):.1f}%"
            )
        print(f"Maximum turn reached:   {int(np.max(turns))}")
        print(f"Cumulative decisions:   {self.total_decisions:,}")
        print(f"Cumulative updates:     {self.total_training_updates:,}")
        print(f"Training updates:       {updates:,}")
        print(f"Elapsed time:           {elapsed:.1f} seconds")
        print(f"Games/second:           {n / elapsed if elapsed > 0 else 0:.2f}")
        print(f"Decisions/second:       {decisions / elapsed if elapsed > 0 else 0:.2f}")
        print(f"Updates/second:         {updates / elapsed if elapsed > 0 else 0:.2f}")
        print(f"Current epsilon:        {self.agent.epsilon:.4f}")
        print(f"PyTorch CPU threads:    {torch.get_num_threads()}")
        if complete_batches >= 2:
            first_batch = results[:progress_interval]
            last_start = (
                complete_batches - 1
            ) * progress_interval
            last_batch = results[
                last_start:last_start + progress_interval
            ]
            first = batch_stats(first_batch)
            last = batch_stats(last_batch)
            print()
            print("CHANGE OVER RUN")
            print("-" * 70)
            print(
                f"First batch: "
                f"{first_batch[0]['game']:,}–"
                f"{first_batch[-1]['game']:,}"
            )
            print(
                f"Last batch:  "
                f"{last_batch[0]['game']:,}–"
                f"{last_batch[-1]['game']:,}"
            )
            print()
            print(
                f"{'Metric':<25}"
                f"{'First Batch':>14}"
                f"{'Last Batch':>14}"
                f"{'Change':>14}"
            )
            print("-" * 67)
            print(
                f"{'Average turn':<25}"
                f"{first['avg']:>14.2f}"
                f"{last['avg']:>14.2f}"
                f"{last['avg'] - first['avg']:>+14.2f}"
            )
            for label, key in [
                ("Games reaching turn 20", "p20"),
                ("Games reaching turn 30", "p30"),
                ("Games reaching turn 40", "p40"),
            ]:
                print(
                    f"{label:<25}"
                    f"{first[key]:>13.1f}%"
                    f"{last[key]:>13.1f}%"
                    f"{last[key] - first[key]:>+13.1f}pp"
                )
            relative_change = (
                100 * (last["avg"] - first["avg"])
                / first["avg"]
                if first["avg"] else 0.0
            )
            print()
            print(
                f"Average turn relative change: "
                f"{relative_change:+.1f}%"
            )
        else:
            print()
            print(
                "First-vs-last comparison requires "
                "at least two complete batches."
            )
        print("=" * 70)
        print()
    def print_learning_summary(self, results, interval=100):
        if not results:
            return
        print()
        print("===================== DQN LEARNING PROGRESS SUMMARY =====================")
        print()
        print(
            f"{'Games':<20}"
            f"{'Average Turn':>14}"
            f"{'Turn 20+':>12}"
            f"{'Turn 30+':>12}"
            f"{'Turn 40+':>12}"
        )
        print("-" * 70)
        averages = []
        batch_stats = []
        for start in range(0, len(results), interval):
            batch = results[start:start + interval]
            turns = [r["turn_reached"] for r in batch]
            n = len(turns)
            avg_turn = sum(turns) / n
            pct_20 = 100 * sum(t >= 20 for t in turns) / n
            pct_30 = 100 * sum(t >= 30 for t in turns) / n
            pct_40 = 100 * sum(t >= 40 for t in turns) / n
            averages.append(avg_turn)
            batch_stats.append({
                "avg": avg_turn,
                "pct_20": pct_20,
                "pct_30": pct_30,
                "pct_40": pct_40,
                "n": n,
            })
            first_game = batch[0]["game"]
            last_game = batch[-1]["game"]
            game_range = f"{first_game}–{last_game}"
            print(
                f"{game_range:<20}"
                f"{avg_turn:>14.2f}"
                f"{pct_20:>11.1f}%"
                f"{pct_30:>11.1f}%"
                f"{pct_40:>11.1f}%"
            )
        all_turns = [r["turn_reached"] for r in results]
        total_games = len(all_turns)
        overall_avg = sum(all_turns) / total_games
        overall_20 = 100 * sum(t >= 20 for t in all_turns) / total_games
        overall_30 = 100 * sum(t >= 30 for t in all_turns) / total_games
        overall_40 = 100 * sum(t >= 40 for t in all_turns) / total_games
        print("-" * 70)
        print(
            f"{'OVERALL':<20}"
            f"{overall_avg:>14.2f}"
            f"{overall_20:>11.1f}%"
            f"{overall_30:>11.1f}%"
            f"{overall_40:>11.1f}%"
        )
        first = batch_stats[0]
        last = batch_stats[-1]
        avg_change = last["avg"] - first["avg"]
        avg_pct_change = (
            100 * avg_change / first["avg"]
            if first["avg"] != 0
            else 0
        )
        change_20 = last["pct_20"] - first["pct_20"]
        change_30 = last["pct_30"] - first["pct_30"]
        change_40 = last["pct_40"] - first["pct_40"]
        print()
        print("======================= CHANGE OVER RUN =======================")
        print()
        print(
            f"{'Metric':<25}"
            f"{'First Batch':>14}"
            f"{'Last Batch':>14}"
            f"{'Change':>14}"
        )
        print("-" * 67)
        print(
            f"{'Average turn':<25}"
            f"{first['avg']:>14.2f}"
            f"{last['avg']:>14.2f}"
            f"{avg_change:>+14.2f}"
        )
        print(
            f"{'Games reaching turn 20':<25}"
            f"{first['pct_20']:>13.1f}%"
            f"{last['pct_20']:>13.1f}%"
            f"{change_20:>+13.1f}pp"
        )
        print(
            f"{'Games reaching turn 30':<25}"
            f"{first['pct_30']:>13.1f}%"
            f"{last['pct_30']:>13.1f}%"
            f"{change_30:>+13.1f}pp"
        )
        print(
            f"{'Games reaching turn 40':<25}"
            f"{first['pct_40']:>13.1f}%"
            f"{last['pct_40']:>13.1f}%"
            f"{change_40:>+13.1f}pp"
        )
        print()
        print(
            f"Average turn relative change: {avg_pct_change:+.1f}%"
        )
        print("===============================================================") 
    def log_game(self, result, path="core/ai/dqn/logs/training_history.csv"):
        path = Path(path)
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        fields = [
            "game",
            "reward",
            "decisions",
            "result",
            "epsilon",
            "average_loss",
            "turn_reached",
        ]
        new_file = not path.exists() or path.stat().st_size == 0
        with path.open("a", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            writer = csv.DictWriter(file, fieldnames=fields)
            if new_file:
                writer.writeheader()
            writer.writerow({field: result.get(field) for field in fields})
    def cleanup_old_checkpoints(
        self,
        path="core/ai/dqn/checkpoints/normandy_latest.pt"
    ):
        import re
        checkpoint_dir = Path(path).parent
        numbered_checkpoints = []
        for file in checkpoint_dir.glob("normandy_*.pt"):
            match = re.fullmatch(r"normandy_(\d+)\.pt", file.name)
            if match:
                numbered_checkpoints.append(
                    (int(match.group(1)), file)
                )
        if len(numbered_checkpoints) <= 1:
            return
        numbered_checkpoints.sort(reverse=True)
        latest_number, latest_file = numbered_checkpoints[0]
        print(f"Keeping checkpoint: {latest_file.name}")
        for number, file in numbered_checkpoints[1:]:
            try:
                file.unlink()
                print(f"Deleted old checkpoint: {file.name}")
            except OSError as error:
                print(
                    f"WARNING: Could not delete {file.name}: {error}"
                )
    def save_checkpoint(self, path="core/ai/dqn/checkpoints/normandy_latest.pt"):
        import re
        path = Path(path)
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        checkpoint_dir = path.parent
        existing_numbers = []
        for file in checkpoint_dir.glob("normandy_*.pt"):
            match = re.fullmatch(r"normandy_(\d+)\.pt", file.name)
            if match:
                existing_numbers.append(int(match.group(1)))
        next_number = max(existing_numbers, default=0) + 1
        while True:
            checkpoint_path = checkpoint_dir / f"normandy_{next_number}.pt"
            try:
                with checkpoint_path.open("xb") as checkpoint_file:
                    checkpoint = {
                        "version": 1,
                        "policy_net": self.agent.policy_net.state_dict(),
                        "target_net": self.agent.target_net.state_dict(),
                        "optimizer": self.agent.optimizer.state_dict(),
                        "replay_buffer": list(self.agent.replay_buffer.buffer),
                        "epsilon": self.agent.epsilon,
                        "training_steps": self.agent.training_steps,
                        "games_completed": self.games_completed,
                        "total_decisions": self.total_decisions,
                        "total_training_updates": self.total_training_updates,
                        "train_frequency": self.agent.train_frequency,
                    }
                    torch.save(checkpoint, checkpoint_file)
                break
            except FileExistsError:
                next_number += 1
            except Exception:
                try:
                    checkpoint_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                except OSError:
                    pass
                raise
        print(
            f"Checkpoint saved: {checkpoint_path} "
            f"(game {self.games_completed})"
        )
        return checkpoint_path
    def load_checkpoint(self, path="core/ai/dqn/checkpoints/normandy_latest.pt"):
        import re
        path = Path(path)
        checkpoint_dir = path.parent
        numbered_checkpoints = []
        for file in checkpoint_dir.glob("normandy_*.pt"):
            match = re.fullmatch(r"normandy_(\d+)\.pt", file.name)
            if match:
                numbered_checkpoints.append(
                    (int(match.group(1)), file)
                )
        numbered_checkpoints.sort(reverse=True)
        candidates = [file for _, file in numbered_checkpoints]
        legacy_path = checkpoint_dir / "normandy_latest.pt"
        if legacy_path.is_file() and legacy_path not in candidates:
            candidates.append(legacy_path)
        if not candidates:
            raise FileNotFoundError(
                f"No checkpoints found in: {checkpoint_dir}"
            )
        for checkpoint_path in candidates:
            try:
                checkpoint = torch.load(
                    checkpoint_path,
                    map_location=self.agent.device,
                    weights_only=False,
                )
                if checkpoint["version"] != 1:
                    raise ValueError("Unsupported checkpoint version")
                if checkpoint["train_frequency"] != self.agent.train_frequency:
                    print(
                        f"Training frequency changed: "
                        f"{checkpoint['train_frequency']} -> "
                        f"{self.agent.train_frequency}"
                    )
                self.agent.policy_net.load_state_dict(
                    checkpoint["policy_net"]
                )
                self.agent.target_net.load_state_dict(
                    checkpoint["target_net"]
                )
                self.agent.target_net.eval()
                self.agent.optimizer.load_state_dict(
                    checkpoint["optimizer"]
                )
                self.agent.replay_buffer.buffer.clear()
                self.agent.replay_buffer.buffer.extend(
                    checkpoint["replay_buffer"]
                )
                self.agent.epsilon = checkpoint["epsilon"]
                self.agent.training_steps = checkpoint["training_steps"]
                self.games_completed = checkpoint["games_completed"]
                self.total_decisions = checkpoint["total_decisions"]
                self.total_training_updates = checkpoint["total_training_updates"]
                print(
                    f"Checkpoint loaded: {checkpoint_path} "
                    f"(game {self.games_completed}, "
                    f"epsilon {self.agent.epsilon:.4f})"
                )
                return checkpoint_path
            except (
                OSError,
                EOFError,
                RuntimeError,
                ValueError,
                KeyError,
                TypeError,
                pickle.UnpicklingError,
            ) as error:
                print(
                    f"WARNING: Could not load checkpoint "
                    f"{checkpoint_path}: {error}"
                )
                print("Trying previous checkpoint...")
        raise RuntimeError(
            "No valid checkpoint could be loaded."
        )
    def train(
        self,
        num_games=10,
        resume=False,
        checkpoint_path=None,
        save_interval=5000,
        overwrite=False,
        num_workers=4,
        progress_interval=200,
        evaluation_interval=500,
        evaluation_games=1,
        greedy_mode=False,
        save_checkpoints=True
    ):
        if num_games < 1:
            raise ValueError("num_games must be at least 1")
        if num_workers < 1:
            raise ValueError("num_workers must be at least 1")
        if progress_interval < 1:
            raise ValueError("progress_interval must be at least 1")
        if evaluation_interval < 1:
            raise ValueError("evaluation_interval must be at least 1")
        if evaluation_games < 1:
            raise ValueError("evaluation_games must be at least 1")
        checkpointing = checkpoint_path is not None
        if checkpointing:
            checkpoint_path = Path(checkpoint_path)
            if resume:
                try:                    
                    self.load_checkpoint(checkpoint_path)
                    self.agent.epsilon = 0.2
                    print(f"Epsilon restarted at: {self.agent.epsilon:.4f}")
                except FileNotFoundError:
                    print("No checkpoint found. Starting fresh.")
            elif checkpoint_path.exists() and not overwrite:
                raise FileExistsError(
                    f"Checkpoint already exists: {checkpoint_path}. "
                    "Use resume=True or overwrite=True."
                )
        start_turn = 1
        previous_end_turn = None
        if MIDGAME_CONFIG["enabled"]:
            snapshot_path = Path(MIDGAME_CONFIG["fixed_snapshot"])
            with snapshot_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                snapshot_metadata = json.load(f)["metadata"]
            start_turn = int(snapshot_metadata["start_turn"])
            previous_end_turn = int(snapshot_metadata["original_final_turn"])
            self.previous_end_turn = previous_end_turn
        window_start_checkpoint = self.games_completed
        if greedy_mode:
            self.agent.epsilon = 0
            print("GREEDY MODE: epsilon = 0")
        results = []
        evaluation_results = []
        turns_in_window = []
        window_start_game = self.games_completed + 1
        run_start = perf_counter()
        last_save_game = 0
        updates_before = self.total_training_updates
        decisions_before = self.total_decisions
        completed = 0
        next_evaluation = evaluation_interval
        episode_rewards = np.zeros(
            num_workers, dtype=np.float64
        )
        episode_decisions = np.zeros(
            num_workers, dtype=np.int64
        )
        episode_losses = [
            [] for _ in range(num_workers)
        ]
        needs_reset = np.zeros(
            num_workers, dtype=bool
        )
        hyperparameters = load_hyperparameters("normandy_v1")
        game_logging_config = hyperparameters.get(
            "game_logging", {"enabled": False}
        )
        error_logging_config = hyperparameters.get(
            "error_logging", {"enabled": False}
        )
        log_manager = multiprocessing.Manager()
        shared_count = log_manager.Value("i", 0)
        shared_lock = log_manager.Lock()
        env_factory = partial(
            make_silent_normandy_env,
            game_config=game_logging_config,
            error_config=error_logging_config,
            shared_count=shared_count,
            shared_lock=shared_lock,
        )
        env = AsyncVectorEnv(
            [env_factory for _ in range(num_workers)],
            autoreset_mode=AutoresetMode.NEXT_STEP,
        )
        try:
            observations, info = env.reset()
            while completed < num_games:
                masks = info["action_mask"]
                actions = self.agent.select_actions(
                    observations,
                    masks,
                    needs_reset,
                )
                (
                    next_observations,
                    rewards,
                    terminated,
                    truncated,
                    next_info,
                ) = env.step(actions)
                next_masks = next_info["action_mask"]
                for i in range(num_workers):
                    if needs_reset[i]:
                        needs_reset[i] = False
                        episode_rewards[i] = 0.0
                        episode_decisions[i] = 0
                        episode_losses[i] = []
                        continue
                    done = bool(
                        terminated[i] or truncated[i]
                    )
                    self.agent.replay_buffer.add(
                        observation=observations[i],
                        action_id=int(actions[i]),
                        reward=float(rewards[i]),
                        next_observation=next_observations[i],
                        terminated=done,
                        next_action_mask=next_masks[i],
                    )
                    loss = None
                    if (
                        (self.total_decisions + 1)
                        % self.agent.train_frequency
                        == 0
                    ):
                        start = perf_counter()
                        loss = self.agent.train_step()
                        duration = perf_counter() - start
                        if duration > 1.0:
                            print(
                                f"SLOW TRAINING UPDATE: "
                                f"Worker {i}, "
                                f"Decision {episode_decisions[i] + 1}, "
                                f"Duration {duration:.2f}s"
                            )
                    if loss is not None:
                        episode_losses[i].append(loss)
                        self.total_training_updates += 1
                    if not greedy_mode:
                        self.agent.epsilon = max(
                            self.agent.epsilon_min,
                            self.agent.epsilon * self.agent.epsilon_decay
                        )
                    episode_rewards[i] += float(rewards[i])
                    episode_decisions[i] += 1
                    self.total_decisions += 1
                    if done:
                        needs_reset[i] = True
                        if completed >= num_games:
                            continue
                        completed += 1
                        self.games_completed += 1
                        game_result = None
                        if "result" in next_info:
                            game_result = next_info["result"][i]
                            turn_reached = int(next_info["turn_reached"][i])
                        cards_in_drawn_pile = int(
                            next_info.get(
                                "cards_in_drawn_pile",
                                next_info["turn_reached"]
                            )[i]
                        )
                        result = {
                            "game": self.games_completed,
                            "reward": float(
                                episode_rewards[i]
                            ),
                            "decisions": int(
                                episode_decisions[i]
                            ),
                            "result": game_result,
                            "epsilon": self.agent.epsilon,
                            "average_loss": (
                                float(np.mean(episode_losses[i]))
                                if episode_losses[i]
                                else None
                            ),
                            "turn_reached": turn_reached,
                            "cards_in_drawn_pile": cards_in_drawn_pile,
                        }
                        results.append(result)
                        turns_in_window.append(turn_reached)
                        if len(turns_in_window) == progress_interval:
                            print()
                            print("========== DQN LEARNING PROGRESS ==========")
                            print(
                                f"Games:               "
                                f"{window_start_game}–{self.games_completed}"
                            )
                            print(
                                f"Average turn reached: "
                                f"{np.mean(turns_in_window):.2f}"
                            )
                            early_defeat_pct = 100.0 * sum(
                                r["result"] == "lost" and r["turn_reached"] < 10
                                for r in results[-len(turns_in_window):]
                            ) / len(turns_in_window)
                            print(f"Defeated before turn 10: {early_defeat_pct:.1f}%")
                            n = len(turns_in_window)
                            pct_20 = 100 * sum(t >= 20 for t in turns_in_window) / n
                            pct_30 = 100 * sum(t >= 30 for t in turns_in_window) / n
                            pct_40 = 100 * sum(t >= 40 for t in turns_in_window) / n
                            print(f"Reached turn 20+:     {pct_20:.1f}%")
                            print(f"Reached turn 30+:     {pct_30:.1f}%")
                            print(f"Reached turn 40+:     {pct_40:.1f}%")
                            if MIDGAME_CONFIG["enabled"]:
                                original_end = self.previous_end_turn
                                pct_exceeded = 100 * sum(
                                    t > original_end for t in turns_in_window
                                ) / n
                                print(
                                    f"Exceeded original end ({original_end}): "
                                    f"{pct_exceeded:.1f}%"
                                )
                            print("===========================================")
                            print(
                                f"Maximum turn reached: "
                                f"{max(turns_in_window)}"
                            )
                            print("===========================================")
                            print()
                            turns_in_window.clear()
                            window_start_game = self.games_completed + 1
                        if greedy_mode:
                            csv_path = (
                                "core/ai/dqn/logs/"
                                f"evaluation_checkpoint_{window_start_checkpoint}_eps0.csv"
                            )
                        else:
                            csv_path = "core/ai/dqn/logs/training_history.csv"
                        self.log_game(result, path=csv_path)
                        print(
                            f"Game {self.games_completed:6d} | "
                            f"Worker: {i} | "
                            f"Result: {game_result!s:6s} | "
                            f"Turns: {turn_reached:2d} | "
                            f"Previous End: {previous_end_turn if previous_end_turn is not None else '-'} | "
                            f"Cards Drawn: {cards_in_drawn_pile:2d} | "
                            f"Reward: {episode_rewards[i]:6.1f} | "
                            f"Decisions: {episode_decisions[i]:4d} | "
                            f"Epsilon: {self.agent.epsilon:.4f}"
                        )
                observations = next_observations
                info = next_info
                if completed >= next_evaluation:
                    next_evaluation += evaluation_interval
                    for _ in range(evaluation_games):
                        evaluation = self.evaluate_policy()
                        evaluation["training_games"] = self.games_completed
                        evaluation_results.append(evaluation)
                        self.log_evaluation(
                            self.games_completed,
                            evaluation,
                        )
                        print()
                        print("========== DQN POLICY EVALUATION ==========")
                        print(f"Training games:  {self.games_completed}")
                        print(f"Evaluation turn: {evaluation['turn_reached']}")
                        print(f"Result:          {evaluation['result']}")
                        print("Epsilon:         0.0")
                        print("===========================================")
                if (
                    checkpointing
                    and completed - last_save_game >= save_interval
                    and not greedy_mode
                    and save_checkpoints
                ):
                    self.save_checkpoint(checkpoint_path)
                    last_save_game = completed
                    self.print_checkpoint_performance(
                        results=results,
                        progress_interval=progress_interval,
                        run_start=run_start,
                        decisions_before=decisions_before,
                        updates_before=updates_before,
                        num_workers=num_workers,
                    )
        finally:
            try:
                env.close()
            finally:
                try:
                    self.close_evaluation_pool(force=True)
                finally:
                    if log_manager is not None:
                        log_manager.shutdown()
        elapsed = perf_counter() - run_start
        games_won = sum(
            1 for result in results
            if result["result"] == "won"
        )
        win_rate = 100 * games_won / len(results) if results else 0.0
        games_per_second = (
            len(results) / elapsed
            if elapsed > 0
            else 0.0
        )
        decisions_this_run = (
            self.total_decisions - decisions_before
        )
        decisions_per_second = (
            decisions_this_run / elapsed
            if elapsed > 0
            else 0.0
        )
        updates_this_run = (
            self.total_training_updates - updates_before
        )
        updates_per_second = (
            updates_this_run / elapsed
            if elapsed > 0
            else 0.0
        )
        print()
        print("=" * 65)
        print("DQN TRAINING PERFORMANCE")
        print("=" * 65)
        print(f"Parallel workers:      {num_workers}")
        print(f"Train frequency:       {self.agent.train_frequency}")
        print(f"Games completed:       {len(results)}")
        print(f"Games won:             {games_won}")
        print(f"Win rate:              {win_rate:.3f}%")
        print(f"Cumulative games:      {self.games_completed}")
        print(f"Cumulative decisions:  {self.total_decisions}")
        print(f"Cumulative updates:    {self.total_training_updates}")
        print(f"Elapsed time:          {elapsed:.2f} seconds")
        print(f"Games/second:          {games_per_second:.2f}")
        print(f"Decisions/second:      {decisions_per_second:.2f}")
        print(f"Training updates:      {updates_this_run}")
        print(f"Updates/second:        {updates_per_second:.2f}")
        print(f"Final epsilon:         {self.agent.epsilon:.4f}")
        print(f"PyTorch CPU threads: {torch.get_num_threads()}")
        print("=" * 65)
        if evaluation_results:
            print()
            print("========== DQN POLICY EVALUATION SUMMARY ==========")
            print()
            print(f"{'Training Games':<20} {'Turn Reached':>15} {'Result':>10}")
            for evaluation in evaluation_results:
                print(
                    f"{evaluation['training_games']:<20} "
                    f"{evaluation['turn_reached']:>15} "
                    f"{evaluation['result']:>10}"
                )
            turns = [
                evaluation["turn_reached"]
                for evaluation in evaluation_results
            ]
            print()
            print(f"Evaluation games:   {len(turns)}")
            print(f"Average turns:      {np.mean(turns):.2f}")
            print(f"First evaluation:   {turns[0]}")
            print(f"Last evaluation:    {turns[-1]}")
            print(f"Change:             {turns[-1] - turns[0]:+d} turns")
            print("===================================================")
        if checkpointing and not greedy_mode and save_checkpoints:
            if last_save_game != completed:
                self.save_checkpoint(checkpoint_path)
                self.print_checkpoint_performance(
                    results=results,
                    progress_interval=progress_interval,
                    run_start=run_start,
                    decisions_before=decisions_before,
                    updates_before=updates_before,
                    num_workers=num_workers,
                )
            self.cleanup_old_checkpoints(checkpoint_path)
        return results
if __name__ == "__main__":
    torch.set_num_threads(3)
    trainer = DQNTrainer()
    trainer.train(
        num_games=20000,
        num_workers=8,
        progress_interval=200,
        evaluation_interval=999999999,
        evaluation_games=1,
        resume=True,
        checkpoint_path="core/ai/dqn/checkpoints/normandy_latest.pt",
        save_interval=10000,
        greedy_mode=False,
        save_checkpoints=True
    )

import gymnasium as gym
import numpy as np
from gymnasium import spaces
from core.ai.dqn.dqn_action_adapter import execute_action_phase_decision, get_action_phase_decision, get_hitler_intervention_decision
from core.ai.dqn.dqn_game_state_encoder import OBSERVATION_SIZE, encode_game_state
from core.ai.headless_action_masks import HeadlessDecisionState, get_total_actions
from reset import reset_game
from core.global_game_state import GlobalGameState
from core.ai.normandy_full_game_ai_dqn import advance_to_next_decision
from core.ai.dqn.dqn_action_adapter import (
    execute_hitler_intervention_decision,
)
from pathlib import Path
import random
from uuid import uuid4
from core.ai.dqn.dqn_save_load import MidgameCollector
from core.ai.dqn.midgame_config import MIDGAME_CONFIG
from core.ai.dqn.dqn_save_load import load_game
from core.ai.headless_action_masks import (
    german_eligible_spaces,
    panzer_divisions_list,
)
from core.allied_armies import armies_list
def debug_action_mask(decision_type, mask):
    legal_ids = np.flatnonzero(mask == 1.0).tolist()
    for action_id in legal_ids:
        if decision_type == "action_phase":
            if action_id == 0:
                description = "END TURN / PASS"
            elif 1 <= action_id <= len(armies_list):
                army = armies_list[action_id - 1]
                description = f"COUNTERATTACK: {army.name}"
            elif 7 <= action_id < (
                7 + len(panzer_divisions_list)
                * len(german_eligible_spaces)
            ):
                relative_id = action_id - 7
                panzer_index = (
                    relative_id // len(german_eligible_spaces)
                )
                space_index = (
                    relative_id % len(german_eligible_spaces)
                )
                panzer = panzer_divisions_list[panzer_index]
                space = german_eligible_spaces[space_index]
                description = (
                    f"DEPLOY PANZER: {panzer.name} -> {space.name}"
                )
            else:
                description = "OTHER ACTION"
        elif decision_type == "hitler_intervention":
            description = (
                "HITLER INTERVENTION ACTION "
                "(interpretation depends on intervention stage)"
            )
        else:
            description = "UNKNOWN DECISION TYPE"
class NormandyEnv(gym.Env):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        total_actions, _ = get_total_actions()
        self.observation_space = spaces.Box(
            low=0.0,
            high=1.0,
            shape=(OBSERVATION_SIZE,),
            dtype=np.float32,
        )
        self.action_space = spaces.Discrete(total_actions)
        self.current_decision_type = "action_phase"
        self.midgame_collector = MidgameCollector(config={
            **MIDGAME_CONFIG, "start_turns": [20],
        })
    def pad_action_mask(self, mask):
        if len(mask) > self.action_space.n:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Action mask has {len(mask)} actions, "
                f"but environment supports only {self.action_space.n}"
            )
        padded_mask = np.zeros(self.action_space.n, dtype=np.float32)
        padded_mask[:len(mask)] = mask
        return padded_mask
    def reset(self, *, seed=None, options=None):
        super().reset(seed=seed)
        self.draw_deck, self.mid_deck, self.late_deck = reset_game()
        HeadlessDecisionState.reset_hitler_intervention()
        self.current_decision_type = "action_phase"
        self.midgame_collector.start_game(uuid4().hex)
        if MIDGAME_CONFIG["enabled"]:
            load_game(self, MIDGAME_CONFIG["fixed_snapshot"])
        if self.current_decision_type == "hitler_intervention":
            observation, mask = get_hitler_intervention_decision(
                GlobalGameState.current_card
            )
        else:
            observation, mask = get_action_phase_decision()
        self.midgame_collector.consider(self)
        return observation, {
            "action_mask": self.pad_action_mask(mask)
        }
    def execute_action(self, action_id):
        if self.current_decision_type == "hitler_intervention":
            return execute_hitler_intervention_decision(
                action_id,
                GlobalGameState.current_card,
                GlobalGameState.current_weather,
            )
        return execute_action_phase_decision(action_id)
    def step(self, action_id):
        reward = 0.0
        terminated = False
        truncated = False
        cards_before = GlobalGameState.cards_drawn
        if self.current_decision_type == "hitler_intervention":
            _, mask = get_hitler_intervention_decision(
                GlobalGameState.current_card
            )
        else:
            _, mask = get_action_phase_decision()
        if (
            action_id < 0
            or action_id >= len(mask)
            or mask[action_id] != 1.0
        ):
            raise ValueError(f"Illegal action: {action_id}")
        result = self.execute_action(action_id)
        if self.current_decision_type == "hitler_intervention":
            if result:
                observation, mask = get_hitler_intervention_decision(
                    GlobalGameState.current_card
                )
            else:
                self.current_decision_type = "action_phase"
                if (
                    GlobalGameState.actions_left_this_turn == 0
                    and GlobalGameState.reserve_actions == 0
                ):
                    decision = advance_to_next_decision(
                        self.draw_deck,
                        self.mid_deck,
                        self.late_deck,
                    )
                    if "result" in decision:
                        terminated = True
                        reward = float(
                            sum(
                                1.12 ** t
                                for t in range(cards_before + 1, GlobalGameState.cards_drawn + 1)
                            )
                        )
                        if decision["result"] == "won":
                            reward += 10000.0
                        self.current_decision_type = None
                        observation, _ = get_action_phase_decision()
                        mask = np.zeros(
                            self.action_space.n,
                            dtype=np.float32,
                        )
                        self.midgame_collector.finish(
                            final_turn=GlobalGameState.cards_drawn,
                            result=decision["result"],
                        )
                        return (
                            observation,
                            reward,
                            terminated,
                            truncated,
                            {
                                "result": decision["result"],
                                "action_mask": mask,
                                "turn_reached": int(GlobalGameState.cards_drawn),
                                "cards_in_drawn_pile": len(GlobalGameState.drawn_cards),
                            },
                        )
                    self.current_decision_type = decision["decision_type"]
                    observation = decision["observation"]
                    mask = decision["action_mask"]
                else:
                    observation, mask = get_action_phase_decision()
        elif (
            GlobalGameState.actions_left_this_turn == 0
            and GlobalGameState.reserve_actions == 0
        ):
            decision = advance_to_next_decision(
                self.draw_deck,
                self.mid_deck,
                self.late_deck,
            )
            if "result" in decision:
                terminated = True
                reward = float(
                        sum(
                            1.12 ** t
                            for t in range(cards_before + 1, GlobalGameState.cards_drawn + 1)
                        )
                )
                if decision["result"] == "won":
                    reward += 10000.0
                self.current_decision_type = None
                observation, _ = get_action_phase_decision()
                mask = np.zeros(
                    self.action_space.n,
                    dtype=np.float32,
                )
                self.midgame_collector.finish(
                    final_turn=GlobalGameState.cards_drawn,
                    result=decision["result"],
                )
                return (
                    observation,
                    reward,
                    terminated,
                    truncated,
                    {
                        "result": decision["result"],
                        "action_mask": mask,
                        "turn_reached": int(GlobalGameState.cards_drawn),
                        "cards_in_drawn_pile": len(GlobalGameState.drawn_cards),
                    },
                )
            self.current_decision_type = decision["decision_type"]
            observation = decision["observation"]
            mask = decision["action_mask"]
        else:
            observation, mask = get_action_phase_decision()
        reward = float(
                sum(
                    1.12 ** t
                    for t in range(cards_before + 1, GlobalGameState.cards_drawn + 1)
                )
        )
        self.midgame_collector.consider(self)
        return (
            observation,
            reward,
            terminated,
            truncated,
            {
                "action_mask": self.pad_action_mask(mask),
            },
        )


============================================================
PATH: core\ai\dqn\normandy_logging_env.py
============================================================
import contextlib
import io
import os
import re
import traceback
import uuid
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from core.ai.dqn.normandy_env import NormandyEnv
from core.global_game_state import GlobalGameState
class NormandyLoggingEnv(NormandyEnv):
    def __init__(
        self,
        config,
        shared_count=None,
        shared_lock=None,
        error_config=None,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.log_config = config or {}
        self.error_config = error_config or {}
        self.shared_count = shared_count
        self.shared_lock = shared_lock
        self.log_buffer = None
        self.game_number = 0
        self.last_action = None
    def _normal_enabled(self):
        return bool(self.log_config.get("enabled", False))
    def _error_enabled(self):
        return bool(self.error_config.get("enabled", False))
    def _limit_reached(self):
        if not self._normal_enabled():
            return True
        if self.shared_count is None:
            return False
        with self.shared_lock:
            return (
                self.shared_count.value
                >= self.log_config.get("max_files", 1)
            )
    def _discard(self):
        if self.log_buffer is not None:
            self.log_buffer.close()
            self.log_buffer = None
    def _target_turns(self):
        target = self.log_config.get("target_turn")
        if target is None:
            return None
        if isinstance(target, (list, tuple)):
            return target
        return [target]
    def _capture(self):
        return self.log_buffer is not None
    def reset(self, *, seed=None, options=None):
        self._discard()
        self.game_number += 1
        self.last_action = None
        capture_needed = (
            self._error_enabled()
            or (
                self._normal_enabled()
                and not self._limit_reached()
            )
        )
        if capture_needed:
            self.log_buffer = io.StringIO()
        try:
            if self._capture():
                with contextlib.redirect_stdout(self.log_buffer):
                    return super().reset(
                        seed=seed,
                        options=options,
                    )
            return super().reset(
                seed=seed,
                options=options,
            )
        except BaseException as error:
            self._handle_error(error, "reset")
            raise
    def step(self, action):
        self.last_action = action
        try:
            if not self._capture():
                return super().step(action)
            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(self.log_buffer):
                result = super().step(action)
            _, _, terminated, truncated, info = result
            if terminated or truncated:
                self._finish_log(info)
                self._discard()
            elif not self._error_enabled():
                targets = self._target_turns()
                max_target = max(targets) if targets else None
                if self._limit_reached() or (
                    max_target is not None
                    and GlobalGameState.cards_drawn > max_target
                ):
                    self._discard()
            return result
        except BaseException as error:
            self._handle_error(error, "step")
            raise
    def _handle_error(self, error, operation):
        try:
            if self._error_enabled():
                self._save_error(error, operation)
        except Exception as logging_error:
            import sys
            print(
                f"ERROR: Could not save crash log: "
                f"{logging_error}",
                file=sys.__stderr__,
            )
        finally:
            self._discard()
    def _save_error(self, error, operation):
        output_dir = Path(
            self.error_config.get(
                "output_dir",
                "core/ai/dqn/logs/errors",
            )
        )
        output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime(
            "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f"
        )
        filename = (
            f"error_{timestamp}_"
            f"pid_{os.getpid()}_"
            f"{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.txt"
        )
        path = output_dir / filename
        log_text = (
            self.log_buffer.getvalue()
            if self.log_buffer is not None
            else "[No game history captured]\n"
        )
        log_text = re.sub(
            r"\x1b\[[0-9;]*m",
            "",
            log_text,
        )
        card = getattr(
            GlobalGameState,
            "current_card",
            None,
        )
        card_id = getattr(card, "card_id", None)
        turn = getattr(
            GlobalGameState,
            "cards_drawn",
            None,
        )
        exception_text = "".join(
            traceback.format_exception(
                type(error),
                error,
                error.__traceback__,
            )
        )
        with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            file.write("NORMANDY DQN ERROR REPORT\n")
            file.write("=" * 70 + "\n")
            file.write(f"Timestamp: {timestamp}\n")
            file.write(f"Worker PID: {os.getpid()}\n")
            file.write(f"Worker-local game: {self.game_number}\n")
            file.write(f"Operation: {operation}\n")
            file.write(f"Turn: {turn}\n")
            file.write(f"Card: {card_id}\n")
            file.write(f"Last action: {self.last_action}\n")
            file.write(
                f"Decision type: "
                f"{self.current_decision_type}\n"
            )
            file.write("\nFULL GAME HISTORY\n")
            file.write("=" * 70 + "\n")
            file.write(log_text)
            file.write("\n\nEXCEPTION TRACEBACK\n")
            file.write("=" * 70 + "\n")
            file.write(exception_text)
    def _finish_log(self, info):
        if not self._normal_enabled():
            return
        if self._limit_reached():
            return
        targets = self._target_turns()
        wanted_result = self.log_config.get("result", "any")
        actual_result = info.get("result")
        turn_reached = info.get("turn_reached")
        matches = (
            (
                wanted_result == "any"
                or actual_result == wanted_result
            )
            and (
                targets is None
                or turn_reached in targets
            )
        )
        if not matches:
            return
        if self.shared_count is None or self.shared_lock is None:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Normal game logging requires a shared "
                "counter and lock."
            )
        with self.shared_lock:
            max_files = self.log_config.get("max_files", 1)
            if self.shared_count.value >= max_files:
                return
            output_dir = Path(
                self.log_config.get(
                    "output_dir",
                    "core/ai/dqn/logs/games",
                )
            )
            output_dir.mkdir(
                parents=True,
                exist_ok=True,
            )
            file_number = self.shared_count.value + 1
            filename = (
                f"game_{file_number:03d}_"
                f"{actual_result}_turn_{turn_reached}_"
                f"pid_{os.getpid()}.txt"
            )
            log_text = self.log_buffer.getvalue()
            log_text = re.sub(
                r"\x1b\[[0-9;]*m",
                "",
                log_text,
            )
            (output_dir / filename).write_text(
                log_text,
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            self.shared_count.value = file_number
    def close(self):
        self._discard()
        return super().close()

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from core.models import AlliedArmy, UnitBox
from core.enums import SideType
class TerrainType(Enum):
    BOCAGE = ("Bocage", 2)
    TOWN = ("Town", 1)
    HILL = ("Hill", 2)
    BEACH = ("Beach", 2)
    FORTRESS = ("Fortress", 4)
    FALAISE_GAP = ("Falaise Gap", 1)
    START_BOX = ("Start Box", 0)
    def __init__(self, display_name, defense_value):
        self.display_name = display_name
        self.defense_value = defense_value
@dataclass
class MapSpace:
    name: str
    track: AlliedArmy
    terrain: TerrainType
    controlling_player: SideType
    terrain_value: int = 0
    track_number: int = 0
    fortified_village_modifier: int = 0
    fortified: bool = False
    model_modifier: int = 0
    under_siege: bool = False
    units: list = field(default_factory=list)
@dataclass
class Track:
    name: str
    spaces: list[MapSpace]
@dataclass
class ResourceTrack:
    name: str
    value: int
    base_level: int
    minimum: int
    maximum: int
transport_track = ResourceTrack(
    name="Transport",
    value=5,
    base_level=3,
    minimum=0,
    maximum=6
)
supply_track = ResourceTrack(
    name="Supply",
    value=4,
    base_level=3,
    minimum=0,
    maximum=6
)
hitler_approval_track = ResourceTrack(
    name="Hitler Approval",
    value=6,
    base_level=3,
    minimum=-2,
    maximum=6
)
falaise_gap = MapSpace(
    name="FALAISE GAP",
    track=None,
    terrain=TerrainType.FALAISE_GAP,
    terrain_value=1,
    track_number=0,
    controlling_player=SideType.GERMAN
)
in_transit_box = UnitBox(
    name="IN TRANSIT"
)
strategic_reserve_box = UnitBox(
    name="STRATEGIC RESERVE"
)
eliminated_units_box = UnitBox(
    name="ELIMINATED UNITS"
)
